## BTC/ETH OI expansion + price-impulse crowding event study — completed (2026-11-12; exploratory, not a trade backtest)

- Motivation: follow the derivatives archive audit with a low-cost predictive event study before building a funding/OI trading strategy. Existing experiments already covered mechanical CRT, liquidity sweep/MSS, displacement, FVG, EMA, Supertrend, breakout and reversal families; this is a new positioning-data hypothesis, not a duplicate indicator matrix.
- Input artifact: two-year BTC/ETH derivatives archive from [workflow 43118448037, artifact 13898484807](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43118448037/artifacts/13898484807). Archive ZIP SHA-256 `65285eda876836c5372f5860014c029e7e0830af0238ef6b06e8dc36e2c4079f`. BTC OI 1h: 17,531 rows, SHA-256 `ae6b8ba594a9fd27fa1139601396aac2b4cf62f72b4679f5f7fea4394a215758`; BTC mark 5m: 210,383 rows, SHA-256 `aa959fda51a577c88611b40e790170547ac50e09ab421ab550f8d21d3793e7b3`; ETH OI 1h: 17,531 rows, SHA-256 `7c8fe703afb875ec1c229283f532012c97cfcd7f435ab21dce90f1b08ace92f8`; ETH mark 5m: 210,383 rows, SHA-256 `8cc2d5d0caa713774932d957dfa7897a1791914df635dbbb76d8f5fe780c38ff`. OI open timestamps span 2024-11-11 12:00 UTC to 2026-11-11 22:00 UTC; mark data spans 2024-11-11 11:20 UTC to 2026-11-11 23:10 UTC. OI and mark series have no internal gaps in this archive.
- Causal alignment: treat each OI 1h timestamp as candle open and expose its close only at timestamp + 1h; mark candle close becomes available at open + 5m. Price alignment uses only the last mark close known at the OI availability timestamp and rejects stale values >5m. OI raw units are not interpreted; only percentage changes are used. Exact OI sampling semantics still require exchange confirmation.
- Main preregistered event: positive 1h OI close-to-close percentage change with rolling z-score >=1.5 versus the prior 168 hourly observations (minimum 72; current observation excluded from mean/std), coincident with an absolute 1h mark-price return at or above the prior 168-observation rolling 95th percentile (threshold shifted one observation). Classify positive price impulse as long-crowding and negative impulse as short-crowding. Apply a global 8h event cooldown across both directions. Measure mark-price forward returns at 1h/4h/8h. A positive direction-adjusted return means reversal; a negative value means continuation of the initial impulse.
- Chronological splits: 70/15/15 by OI-available timestamp; train boundary 2026-04-06 20:00 UTC; validation boundary 2026-07-25 09:30 UTC; first OOS hour after the validation boundary. The main event definition was fixed before split metrics were summarized. Sensitivity grid z=1.0/1.5/2.0 and prior price-tail q90/q95 is descriptive only; no threshold is selected from validation/OOS.
- Main results (mark-price event study; not executable returns):
  - BTC: train 42 events, mean expected 4h reversal -0.046%, reversal-direction hit 45.2%; validation 12 events, -0.161%, hit 41.7%; OOS 10 events, -0.278%, hit 60.0%. OOS hit rate is noisy and does not offset the negative mean (a few large continuation moves dominate).
  - ETH: train 39 events, mean expected 4h reversal -0.460%, hit 41.0%; validation 4 events, +0.429%, hit 75.0% (too small to interpret); OOS 9 events, -0.487%, hit 44.4%.
  - Price-only prior-q95 control, with the same 8h cooldown: BTC train/validation/OOS had 403/88/91 events and reversal hits 49.1%/53.4%/49.5%; ETH had 413/88/87 events and 46.7%/55.7%/47.1%. The OI filter did not improve reversal direction-hit rates consistently and sharply reduced sample size.
  - Main-spec monthly stability: BTC 12/22 active months had positive mean reversal return (54.5%), but average monthly mean was essentially flat (+0.006%); ETH 11/23 months were positive (47.8%), average monthly mean -0.388%. Sensitivity event counts ranged BTC train 28-133 / validation 8-24 / OOS 7-25 and ETH train 26-125 / validation 4-20 / OOS 4-32. No stable reversal pattern emerged.
- Decision: do not build or promote an OI-expansion reversal strategy from this evidence. The event study is descriptive only: mark price is not executable traded-price OHLC, and it does not model fees, spread, slippage, funding, stops, R, PF, drawdown or losing streak. No strategy profitability claim is made. Funding remains excluded because historical event-time realization/settlement mapping is unresolved; OI raw units and exact sampling convention also need exchange confirmation.
- Reproducible code: `research/oi_crowding_event_study.py` (commits `bf6deb3b94bae6b7ed6ec6f4be9eaa832c9f6ced` and output-directory fix `68e648298c7afd983e172da59e9d6db16a6dd8e5`). The script validates manifest hashes/row counts and cadence, exports aligned features, main events, monthly, sensitivity and summary CSVs. Four deterministic self-tests cover no future mark close, future-data perturbation invariance, global event cooldown, and outcome-independent event selection; local self-test passed. The full local rerun reproduced the summary from the archived CSVs.
- Next: (1) use Delta's public funding_rate WebSocket payload fields `fi`, `fr`, `nfr`, `ts` to capture current BTC/ETH per-symbol interval and next-realization metadata; (2) keep historical funding out of P&L until an event-level settlement cross-check is available; (3) separately preregister an OI-contraction/deleveraging event study, without reusing this failed OI-expansion reversal result to tune parameters. If a later signal survives, move to traded-price OHLC and a true cost/risk backtest.


## BTC OI contraction + price-impulse traded-price backtest — completed, rejected (2026-11-12)

- New experiment distinct from the earlier mark-price OI-expansion event study: executable BTCUSD traded-price 15m OHLC backtest with completed 1h OI signals. Code: research/oi_contraction_traded_backtest.py; workflow: .github/workflows/oi-contraction-btc-backtest.yml; report: quant_discovery/BTC_OI_CONTRACTION_TRADE_BACKTEST_2026-11-12.md.
- Inputs: BTC OI 1h 17,531 rows, SHA-256 ae6b8ba594a9fd27fa1139601396aac2b4cf62f72b4679f5f7fea4394a215758; BTCUSD traded 15m 70,079 rows, SHA-256 a21f6fb1e90c4dbabf19bae0df1a891e9549c7ce9eb89528dd3ba1fca2de348a. OI spans 2024-11-11 12:00 to 2026-11-11 22:00 UTC; traded 15m spans 2024-11-11 23:30 to 2026-11-11 23:00 UTC. Both intervals passed strict cadence checks with no gaps. Valid feature rows after warm-up/alignment: 17,446, 2024-11-15 02:00 to 2026-11-11 23:00 UTC. Chronological boundaries: train through 2026-04-07 21:30 UTC; validation through 2026-07-25 22:15 UTC; OOS follows.
- Signal: completed 1h OI close-to-close pct change <0; prior-168h z-score <= -1.5/-2.5; absolute traded-price 1h close return >= prior-168h q90/q95. OI candle close is exposed at open+1h; signal enters next 15m open. Direction continuation/reversal; ATR14 Wilder from prior completed 15m candle; 1.0/1.5 ATR stops; 1.5/2/3R targets; 32-bar (8h) max hold; one position per config; 8h event cooldown. Matrix: 48 configurations.
- Execution: 12bp assumed round-trip primary; 6/12/20bp sensitivity; same-bar SL first; adverse stop gap at worse open; target gap at target; time exit at final 15m close; no funding. Training gate >=40 trades, validation >=20; PF>1 and net R>0 in both; OOS only for candidates passing both.
- Raw-data primary result: 1 training-eligible, 0 validation passes; OOS not evaluated. Only training-eligible config: z<=-2.5/q90, reversal, 1.5 ATR stop, 1.5R target. Train 128 trades, 51.56% wins, PF 1.047, +3.49R, avg +0.027R, DD -15.36R, losing streak 7, 47.1% positive months. Matching validation: 36 trades, 50.00% wins, PF 0.823, -3.75R, avg -0.104R, DD -7.26R, losing streak 2, 25.0% positive months.
- Raw candidate cost sensitivity: 6bp train +17.61R/PF1.263 and validation +0.74R/PF1.039; 12bp train +3.49R/PF1.047 and validation -3.75R/PF0.823; 20bp train -15.33R/PF0.815 and validation -9.73R/PF0.597.
- A separate post-inspection data-quality sensitivity excluded abs OI pct >20% or abs 1h traded-price return >5%; this filter is not preregistered and is not primary evidence. It produced 5 training-eligible and 4 validation-pass candidates, but all four failed OOS at 12bp: -14.61R to -16.53R, PF0.338–0.396, 25–27 trades, DD -16.92R to -24.40R, losing streak 7. Best filtered validation candidate (z<=-2.5/q95, reversal, 1.5 ATR, 2R) was train 53 trades/+2.64R/PF1.073; validation 26/+6.06R/PF1.490; OOS 25/-15.11R/PF0.352, win 24%, DD -18.84R, losing streak 7. Its OOS monthly results were Jul +0.26R, Aug -3.61R, Sep -4.95R, Oct -6.81R; OOS regime results were negative in downtrend, range and uptrend. Do not tune this inspected validation/OOS period.
- Raw OI hourly pct changes include extremes (BTC min approximately -57.8%, max +114.9%); absolute units/sampling semantics are unverified. Do not infer liquidation events from these jumps without reconciliation. Funding remains excluded pending event-level settlement mapping. Traded-price OHLC is used for signal and fills; mark price is not used.
- Six deterministic execution-order self-tests passed; script compilation and local raw/filtered matrix execution passed against the exact archived CSVs. A dedicated GitHub Actions workflow now downloads two years of BTCUSD traded 15m and OI data, runs raw primary plus filtered sensitivity, and retains hashes/results as artifacts.
- Decision: reject the mechanical OI-contraction/price-impulse strategy. Next: reconcile OI series units/sampling against Delta ticker data and an independent source before designing another derivatives-positioning strategy.

## Independent Binance OI sanity check and Delta series-reconciliation tooling — 2026-11-12

- Motivation: the Delta BTC OI:BTCUSD history used by the prior OI-contraction study contains extreme hourly percentage changes (approximately -57.8% to +114.9%). The strategy was already rejected; this step pauses parameter research and checks whether an independent venue shows comparable short-horizon discontinuities.
- Independent source: Binance USDⓈ-M futures open-interest statistics for BTCUSDT and ETHUSDT, 1h period, 500 observations per contract, retrieved 2026-11-12 around 07:57 UTC. This is a separate venue and contract universe; it is not a unit-for-unit substitute for Delta OI.
- BTCUSDT: 500 hourly observations from 2026-10-22 12:00 UTC through 2026-11-12 07:00 UTC. OI quantity rose from 93,083.799 to 98,358.722 base-asset units (+5.667% over the sample). Largest hourly increase +2.330% at 2026-11-05 17:00 UTC; largest hourly decrease -3.059% at 2026-11-05 16:00 UTC; 95th percentile absolute hourly change 0.982%; zero hourly changes exceeded 5%. A contemporaneous Binance current-OI snapshot was 98,324.878, -0.034% versus the latest completed hourly statistic.
- ETHUSDT: 500 hourly observations over the same window. OI quantity rose from 2,292,600.293 to 2,308,186.669 base-asset units (+0.680%). Largest hourly increase +1.411% at 2026-10-22 15:00 UTC; largest hourly decrease -4.351% at 2026-11-08 22:00 UTC; 95th percentile absolute hourly change 0.678%; zero hourly changes exceeded 5%. Contemporaneous current-OI snapshot was 2,309,865.920, +0.073% versus the latest completed hourly statistic.
- Interpretation: the independent Binance sample shows no >5% hourly OI discontinuities in this recent 500-hour window. This does **not** prove Delta's historical OI candles are wrong: venue coverage, contract design, aggregation and time window differ. It does reinforce that Delta's extreme values must be reconciled before using them as liquidation/deleveraging signals.
- Official Delta documentation was reviewed: the ticker schema defines `oi` as the number of outstanding contracts, `oi_value` as open-interest value in base currency, and `oi_value_usd` as USD-converted value. The historical candles endpoint supports OI-prefixed symbols such as `OI:ETHUSD`, but its public schema does not define the economic unit or sampling convention of those candle values. Sources: [Delta ticker field definitions](https://docs.delta.exchange/) and [historical OI candle symbol documentation](https://docs.delta.exchange/).
- Added read-only reconciliation tooling: `research/oi_semantics_reconciliation.py` and `.github/workflows/oi-semantics-reconciliation.yml`. The audit requests BTCUSD/ETHUSD live tickers plus completed 5m and 1h OI candles, compares each close against ticker `oi`, `oi_value`, and `oi_value_usd`, checks 5m-vs-1h close alignment, preserves response hashes and refuses to classify stale ticker snapshots as matches. A strong match requires both completed resolutions within 5% of the same fresh ticker field; a single-resolution match is only provisional.
- Verification: the branch's deterministic CI run [43179032366](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43179032366) completed successfully, including repository-wide Python compilation and the existing offline test suite. The dedicated live Delta reconciliation workflow's output has not yet been independently verified; no Delta unit/sampling conclusion is claimed from the new script until its report artifact or published JSON is inspected.
- Decision: continue quarantining the raw Delta OI percentage-change feature. Do not run another OI strategy matrix or tune the failed OI-contraction study until the direct Delta ticker/candle reconciliation is verified and repeated snapshots show stable mapping. Funding remains excluded from P&L pending event-time settlement mapping. No production strategy, live orders or execution alerts changed.


## Delta OI unit reconciliation and 5m anomaly drilldown — 2026-11-12

- Research question: are the unusually large BTC/ETH Delta OI hourly changes caused by wrong units or hourly candle aggregation, or are they present in the underlying 5m series?
- Executed live audit: deterministic CI run [43180579326](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43180579326) passed. Artifact [13926042971](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43180579326/artifacts/13926042971) contains response hashes, ticker/product snapshots, 5m/1h OI candles and historical anomaly-window checks.
- Timestamp correction: the live Delta ticker timestamp was returned in microseconds, not seconds. The audit now normalizes seconds/milliseconds/microseconds and computes ticker age against the HTTP Date header. The two latest snapshots were fresh (BTC 2.1s, ETH 0.8s).
- Unit mapping: BTCUSD ticker oi=1,058.938 and oi_value=1,058.938 BTC; ETHUSD ticker oi=19,683.22 and oi_value=19,683.22 ETH. In both snapshots, oi and oi_value are numerically identical. The product specs show contract_value=0.001 BTC for BTCUSD and 0.01 ETH for ETHUSD. Multiplying ticker oi by contract_value gives only 0.1% of oi_value for BTC and 1% for ETH; the base OI implies 1,058,938 BTC contracts and 1,968,322 ETH contracts respectively. Thus the generic docs description of ticker oi as a raw contract count conflicts with the live field values/product conversion. Do not treat ticker oi as contract count. The historical OI candle series matches oi_value (base-asset quantity), not oi_value_usd.
- USD conversion sanity check: oi_value_usd / oi_value implied a price within +0.0489% of mark for BTC and +0.0464% for ETH, supporting oi_value as base-asset quantity and oi_value_usd as USD notional.
- Latest scale check: completed 5m and 1h OI candle closes matched oi_value within +0.065% to +0.483% across BTC/ETH. Twelve recent 1h closes per symbol matched the final 5m close exactly.
- Historical 5m/1h aggregation drilldown: four anomaly windows were checked for both BTCUSD and ETHUSD (2025-12-21 09:00 UTC, 2026-08-19 15:00 UTC, 2026-10-29 14:00 UTC, 2026-11-08 21:00 UTC; two complete hours per window). All 16 complete hourly OHLC bars (8 per symbol) matched the aggregation of the corresponding twelve 5m OHLC bars exactly (0.0% field differences). Therefore the large hourly highs/lows and closes are present in the 5m series; they are not caused by a faulty 1h aggregation.
- Examples of large BTC OI close changes in the archived series: +114.86% at 2025-12-21 09:00 UTC then -52.03% at 10:00; -38.76% at 2026-08-19 15:00; -20.14% at 2026-10-29 14:00 then -14.74% at 15:00; -33.86% at 2026-11-08 21:00 then +2.82% at 22:00. The 5m series contains the corresponding rapid changes (e.g. maximum absolute 5m close change 70.52% in the first 2025-12-21 hour and 23.16% in the 2026-11-08 21:00 hour).
- Cross-venue context: Binance USDⓈ-M BTCUSDT/ETHUSDT 1h OI had zero >5% changes in the latest 500-hour sample. At 2026-11-08 21:00 Delta BTC OI fell 33.86% while Binance BTC OI fell 0.109% and Binance ETH OI fell 0.001%; at 2026-10-29 14:00 Delta BTC fell 20.14% while Binance BTC rose 0.903% and ETH rose 0.243%. These are not market-wide OI moves; they are Delta-specific or Delta-feed-specific and must not be used as a general crypto crowding signal without additional validation.
- Two-year Delta BTC OI archive diagnostics: 17,530 hourly close changes; 4,796 exceeded 5%, 1,650 exceeded 10%, 299 exceeded 20%, seven exceeded 50%, and one exceeded 100%. Absolute OI change had Pearson correlation 0.361 with absolute hourly BTC return, 0.412 with hourly price range and 0.290 with BTC hourly volume. This indicates large OI changes cluster with active/volatile hours but does not prove that every event is economically genuine.
- Decision: treat OI:BTCUSD and OI:ETHUSD historical candle closes as base-asset OI aligned to ticker oi_value. Do not use ticker oi as raw contract count; keep oi_value_usd separate as USD notional. The 1h series is an exact OHLC aggregation of the 5m series in the tested windows, but the rapid 5m changes remain exchange-specific and may include exchange-side accounting or feed behaviour. Keep the previous OI-contraction strategy rejected; do not promote a market-wide OI signal.
- Next step: build a bounded 5m base-OI event dataset for BTCUSD and ETHUSD around large Delta-specific contractions/expansions, pair it with same-time Delta traded-price OHLC and Binance OI/price controls, then run a descriptive event study before any trading backtest. Keep venue-specific and market-wide hypotheses separate. Funding remains excluded from P&L until settlement-event mapping is independently verified.
- Code and CI: research/oi_semantics_reconciliation.py now normalizes ticker timestamp units, compares ticker fields to product contract_value, and checks historical 5m-to-1h OHLC aggregation. The CI self-tests cover timestamp normalization, product conversion, field ambiguity and 5m aggregation. CI run 43180579326 passed, including the live audit and full existing test suite. No strategy, production rule, live order or execution alert changed.


## Delta OI contraction/expansion descriptive event study — 2026-11-12

- Input data: the same verified BTCUSD 1h OI archive (17,531 candles) and BTCUSD 15m traded-price archive (70,079 candles) downloaded from artifact [13924985913](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43177146399/artifacts/13924985913). Price and OI timestamps were aligned to completed hourly closes; no missing hourly intervals were found in the aligned series.
- Hypothesis: a large change in Delta base-asset OI may contain information about subsequent BTC price returns beyond the event-hour price impulse and volatility.
- Rules fixed before comparison: absolute OI close-to-close change thresholds 5%, 10%, 20%; separate contraction/expansion; one selected event per direction/threshold per 8-hour cooldown; chronological 70/15/15 splits; forward returns measured 1h/4h/8h after the event-hour close. Events whose forward horizon crossed a split boundary were excluded from that horizon. This is a descriptive event study, not a trade backtest.
- For the 10% threshold, 8h-spaced event counts were: contraction 463 train / 71 validation / 41 OOS; expansion 411 / 40 / 23. Threshold 20% became sparse in later periods (only 5 validation and 7 OOS contraction events; 5 validation and 1 OOS expansion event).
- 10% contraction, all event-hour price directions: mean 8h forward BTC return +0.081% train (462 valid events), +0.226% validation (71), +0.023% OOS (41); OOS median was -0.117% and only 43.9% of OOS events had positive 8h returns.
- 10% expansion: mean 8h forward return -0.038% train (411), -0.046% validation (40), +0.110% OOS (23); OOS median +0.048%, with only 52.2% positive.
- Price-only matched control: controls were non-event hours matched by event-hour price direction and training-defined quintiles of absolute price return and price range; all raw 10% OI-event hours were excluded from controls. At 8h, contraction event-minus-control mean was +0.058% train, +0.288% validation, but -0.095% OOS (37 matched OOS events; only 43.2% beat their matched control). Expansion differences were -0.036% train, +0.002% validation and +0.039% OOS (23 OOS events; 47.8% beat control).
- Directional split reinforces instability: 10% contraction with a down-price event hour had +0.308% mean 8h forward return in validation but -0.182% OOS (20 events; 35% positive). 10% expansion with an up-price event hour had -0.281% validation mean but +0.400% OOS mean, only 12 OOS events.
- Decision: no stable incremental predictive edge survives the chronological OOS split. The validation uplift for contraction is not reproduced OOS, and expansion results are small/inconsistent. Do not convert these events into a trading rule. No fees, slippage, funding, R, PF or trading drawdown are reported because this is not an executable strategy test.
- Interpretation: Delta OI changes are strongly associated with active hours (two-year Pearson correlation of absolute OI change with absolute hourly BTC return 0.361, hourly range 0.412 and volume 0.290), but the matched-control study shows no reliable OOS advantage beyond price direction/range. Keep the OI contraction strategy rejected.
- Next: collect a bounded 5m base-asset OI sample for BTCUSD/ETHUSD and pair it with same-time traded-price and Binance controls. Pre-register event thresholds/cooldown, retain separate venue-specific vs market-wide hypotheses, and run another descriptive event study before any execution backtest.


## Delta BTCUSD/ETHUSD 5m OI impulse event study — new, preregistered (2026-11-12)

- Research question: determine whether the unusually large Delta OI changes seen in the hourly series are isolated to 1h aggregation or also appear in the underlying 5m base-asset OI series; measure subsequent traded-price/OI behaviour and compare the latest month with Binance USD-M OI/price as an independent venue control. This is descriptive event research, not a trading strategy.
- Data: two years of Delta OI:BTCUSD, OI:ETHUSD 5m and 1h candles plus BTCUSD/ETHUSD traded-price 5m OHLCV. All timestamps are candle opens; a 5m signal is available only at open + 5m. Keep only fully closed candles. 5m OI is interpreted in base-asset units based on the prior live ticker/candle reconciliation, but venue-specific sampling semantics remain a limitation.
- Data-quality checks: retain per-dataset CSV SHA-256 and raw-response SHA-256; row counts, date range, irregular intervals and gaps; compare all complete 12-bar 5m OHLC groups against the independent 1h OI candle series, reporting exact/common-hour match rate. Never fill missing candles; feature and forward-outcome calculations reset at every gap.
- Features: 5m close-to-close OI percentage change; rolling z-score versus the preceding 288 completed 5m changes with current observation excluded (context only, not the primary event selector); same-bar traded-price open-to-close return; prior-24h price regime. Event available time is 5m open + 5m. Forward traded-price and OI close changes are measured at 5m/15m/1h/4h and cannot cross a data gap.
- Preregistered sensitivity: absolute OI-change thresholds 1%, 2%, 5%, 10% crossed with 60-minute and 480-minute global cooldowns per contiguous segment. Main specification is abs 5m OI change >=5%, 60-minute cooldown, with expansion and contraction separated. Chronological 70/15/15 splits. Events and matched controls are excluded if the 4h outcome would cross the relevant train/validation/OOS boundary; this split-boundary guard was added after code review before the run.
- Matched control: for main 5% events, one nearest non-event control within +/-24h, without replacement, matched within the same split on same 5m price direction, same prior-24h regime, and same absolute 5m price-return decile defined using training-only quantiles. Control selection does not inspect forward outcomes.
- Independent venue control: Binance USD-M 1h openInterestHist base-asset notional (sumOpenInterestValue) and 1h klines for BTCUSDT/ETHUSDT over the latest 29 days only. Official Binance documentation states the OI statistics endpoint has a maximum of 500 records per response and only the latest one month is available; therefore this is a recent-period cross-venue control, not a two-year independent history. Delta and Binance OI contracts/venue universes are not economically identical.
- No transaction costs, funding, net R, win rate, profit factor or trading drawdown are reported because no entry/exit strategy is being tested. Funding remains excluded pending event-time settlement reconciliation.
- Code: research/oi_5m_delta_event_study.py, initial commit fb2abf4fa98814a6cbd0c331071cde432b1ca37d, split-boundary safety fix c4c76b33a24ae1fdcfae06b021e80bdfdca7269a; workflow .github/workflows/oi-5m-delta-event-study.yml, commit d03bbe0584c5df57b23ed9a4b079eacf3277cc7c; branch ai-quant-rebuild.
- Local verification before dispatch: py_compile passed; six deterministic tests passed for close-availability timestamps, future perturbation invariance, event cooldown, 4h split-boundary safety, no forward outcome across a missing 5m interval, and complete 5m-to-1h OHLC aggregation. A separate 20,000-row synthetic integration run also exercised event selection and matched-control pairing (33 events, 33 matched pairs); synthetic figures are test fixtures, not market results.
- Dedicated two-year workflow has not yet completed at ledger entry time. Do not report market event counts, data coverage, cross-venue correlation or forward-return findings until its job summary, published JSON and artifact are inspected. No strategy is promoted from this event study.
- Research-only branch; no production/live strategy changes, orders, execution alerts or main modifications.


## BTC intraday — OI contraction trade-backtest data-quality correction (2026-11-12)

- User instruction: prioritize finding a repeatable intraday trading edge; this experiment converts the earlier OI-contraction observation into an explicitly rule-based BTC trade backtest.
- Code audit found that the prior quality-filter branch removed >20% hourly OI jumps only after computing the rolling OI z-score. Such observations could therefore contaminate the rolling baseline even when the event itself was filtered out.
- Correction: non-positive OI levels are now treated as missing in both primary and sensitivity modes. In quality-filter mode, >20% hourly OI close-to-close changes are masked BEFORE rolling z-score estimation; the resulting return to a prior level is also naturally missing. No interpolation or forward fill is used. The primary raw mode retains positive >20% changes as an intentionally unfiltered comparator.
- Added deterministic regression coverage using synthetic hourly OI series with a >20% level jump/snap-back and a zero-OI sentinel; assert those observations are excluded from quality-filter features and zero-level observations are excluded from both modes.
- Existing strategy rules remain: negative 1H OI change with z-score threshold 1.5/2.5 plus 1H traded-price impulse beyond prior 168h q90/q95; continuation vs reversal; ATR(14) stop 1.0/1.5; target 1.5R/2R/3R; 8h event cooldown and max holding; next 15m open; same-bar SL first, adverse stop gap at worse open, target at target; flat cost sensitivity 6/12/20bp; funding excluded.
- Split and selection rules: chronological 70/15/15; training >=40 trades and PF>1/net R>0; validation >=20 trades and PF>1/net R>0; OOS only for a candidate passing both gates.
- Known limitation: OI units/sampling semantics remain under reconciliation; raw and quality-filtered runs are diagnostic experiments, not validated trading strategies. The >20% filter is post-inspection sensitivity, not the primary selection rule.
- Code commit: `49fb1026afaffc21a50ec7aa3ac370b979bd2caf`; triggered workflow by commit-message prefix `RUN_OI_CONTRACTION_BACKTEST:`. Results pending verified GitHub Actions completion.


## BTCUSD 15m Donchian breakout-retest — preregistered price-only experiment (2026-11-12)

- Motivation: the existing immediate-entry Donchian breakout + ATR chandelier was rejected (0 training-qualified candidates); test a distinct entry hypothesis by waiting for the first retest and hold of the broken channel boundary rather than entering the initial breakout close.
- Data: Delta Exchange India BTCUSD traded-price 15m OHLCV, two-year archive via bootstrap_historical_data.py; no OI, mark-price, funding or account data. Record exact CSV SHA-256, row count, date coverage, zero-volume bars and missing intervals.
- Signal: prior 20/40 completed 15m highs/lows (shifted one bar); breakout close beyond boundary with 0/0.1 ATR14 buffer; first retest within 4/8 subsequent bars; retest candle range intersects boundary +/-0.1/0.25 breakout ATR and closes back outside; compare close-hold vs directional-body confirmation. Enter next 15m open. The first retest consumes the setup even if confirmation fails; a close back through the level invalidates it. No overlapping pending breakout event per signal generator.
- Stops: retest extreme +/-0.1 retest ATR or 1.5 retest ATR. Targets 1.5R/2R/3R. Optional completed-1H EMA50 alignment; optional breakout relative volume >=1.5x prior 96-bar median; both/long-only/short-only; all hours or 07:00-19:00 UTC. Maximum 32 15m bars (8h).
- Matrix: 2 channels x 2 breakout buffers x 2 retest tolerances x 2 retest windows x 2 confirmation modes x 2 stops x 3 targets x 2 HTF filters x 3 direction modes x 2 sessions x 2 volume filters = 4,608 configurations.
- Evaluation: chronological 70/15/15; breakout, retest, entry and full 8h holding window must remain inside the same split and contiguous price segment; one position/config; training >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, DD>=-25R and positive months>=50%; top 25 by training net-R/drawdown score; validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, DD>=-15R and positive months>=50%; evaluate OOS once for one validation-selected candidate only.
- Execution/cost: next-open entry; same-bar stop first; adverse stop gap at worse open; fixed target fill; timeout at final 15m close; skip >5% stops; assumed 6/12/20bp round-trip sensitivity (12bp primary), no funding and no separate spread/slippage model; actual account fee tier not verified.
- Code: research/donchian_retest_intraday_backtest.py; workflow: .github/workflows/donchian-retest-intraday-backtest.yml; branch ai-quant-rebuild. Per-configuration monthly/regime attribution is retained. Dedicated workflow must pass compilation, deterministic signal/execution tests, two-year traded-price download, matrix and artifact upload before any result is reported.
- OI research gate: the completed BTC OI contraction trade study had 1 training-eligible candidate and 0 validation passes; the matching candidate lost -3.75R in validation at 12bp. Its post-inspection filtered variants all failed OOS (-14.61R to -16.53R). Latest Delta reconciliation remains resolution-sensitive: the 2026-11-12 15:22 UTC snapshot classified BTC as a provisional single-resolution match (5m close vs base-asset ticker field -0.624%, 1h -6.277%) and ETH as a strong base-asset match with contract-count semantics conflict (5m +0.099%, 1h -2.164%). Product conversion ratios were 0.001 BTC and 0.01 ETH; the audit did not validate raw contract-count interpretation. Large changes remain Delta-specific and were not confirmed by Binance; the matched-control event study found no stable incremental OOS predictive edge. Keep OI out of strategy ranking.
- Status at registration: script committed; workflow trigger pending. No performance result claimed until the dedicated run and artifact are inspected. Experimental only; no production rules, orders, execution alerts or main changes.


### BTCUSD Donchian breakout-retest — completed, rejected (2026-11-12)

- Corrected workflow [43247901464](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43247901464) completed successfully; artifact [13959100931](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43247901464/artifacts/13959100931) was downloaded and inspected. The corrected rerun completed all steps and reproduced the matrix.
- Dataset: Delta BTCUSD traded-price 15m, 70,127 rows, zero gaps, 80 zero-volume bars; 2024-11-12 03:30 UTC to 2026-11-12 15:00 UTC. CSV SHA-256: a5c12eb6f43f131e6101d667597b1da1673c6a8e4e439e8ed9c2263d294cd6f3. Train ends 2026-04-07 11:15 UTC; validation ends 2026-07-26 01:00 UTC.

Training: 87 trades, 51.72% win rate, PF 1.065, +3.38R, average +0.039R, max DD -11.28R, losing streak 7, 53.3% positive active months. Matching validation: 17 trades, 35.29% win rate, PF 0.488, -7.63R, average -0.449R, max DD -8.62R, losing streak 5, 0% positive active months; below the 20-trade minimum and negative.
- Second training candidate: Donchian40, zero breakout buffer, 0.1 ATR retest tolerance, 8-bar window, directional-body, 1.5 ATR stop, 1.5R target, EMA50, short-only, 07:00-19:00 UTC, relative-volume >=1.5. Training: 98 trades, 51.02% win rate, PF 1.052, +3.02R, average +0.031R, DD -13.96R, losing streak 5, 58.8% positive active months. Validation: 17 trades, 29.41% win rate, PF 0.400, -9.56R, average -0.562R, DD -9.56R, losing streak 4, 0% positive active months.
- Both shortlisted candidates were short-only and classified in the bear regime; neither produced a long-side training-qualified candidate. The best validation-only row with >=20 trades was long-only and made +2.13R/PF 1.086 on 29 validation trades, but its matching training segment lost -66.72R/PF 0.563 on 142 trades (DD -66.72R, 5.6% positive months); reject as severe train/validation inconsistency.
- Cost sensitivity confirms fragility. Candidate 1 training/validation net R at 6/12/20bp: +15.24/-4.81R, +3.38/-7.63R, -12.43/-11.38R. Candidate 2: +16.77/-7.03R, +3.02/-9.56R, -15.30/-12.93R. No candidate passed validation at any cost.
- Decision: reject the mechanical breakout-retest model. The small training gains disappear in validation, sample size is below the minimum, and results are highly cost-sensitive. Do not evaluate OOS or tune the inspected validation period.
- Code: research/donchian_retest_intraday_backtest.py; workflow .github/workflows/donchian-retest-intraday-backtest.yml; branch ai-quant-rebuild. A SHA-256 reporting typo was corrected before the successful rerun. Research-only; no production/live rule changes, orders, execution alerts or main changes.

## BTC 5m execution after 1H liquidity sweep + 15m MSS — preregistered study (2026-11-12)

- Motivation: existing mechanical liquidity sweep + MSS studies entered on the next 15m open and failed validation. Existing 5m EMA pullback work is a separate strategy family. This experiment isolates whether a lower-timeframe retest/confirmation improves execution after the same causal 1H sweep + 15m MSS event.
- New code: `research/btc_5m_sweep_mss_execution_backtest.py`; workflow: `.github/workflows/btc-5m-sweep-mss-execution.yml`; branch: `ai-quant-rebuild`.
- Data: download up to 730 days of Delta BTCUSD traded-price 5m OHLCV; derive 15m, 1h and 4h candles only from complete contiguous 5m bars. No interpolation. Report raw row counts, missing intervals, duplicate rows, zero-volume rows and SHA-256.
- External liquidity: confirmed 1H swing high/low with 2 or 3 bars on each side. Crucial causality rule: the pivot becomes usable only on the first 15m close strictly AFTER the 1H right-side confirmation close (`searchsorted(side="right")`); do not use the 15m candle that ends at the same confirmation timestamp.
- Sweep: wick beyond the frozen 1H level and close back across it. One sweep attempt per level; level expires after 72h. Structure reference is the last already-confirmed 15m opposite swing before the sweep.
- MSS: 15m close through that prior confirmed local swing within 4 or 8 bars, while retaining the reclaim. Sweep-to-MSS extreme defines the structural stop reference.
- 5m entry modes: (1) first 5m open after the completed 15m MSS close (baseline); (2) most recent same-direction 5m three-candle FVG formed after sweep and fully closed by MSS, then midpoint touch + directional body/close confirmation, enter next 5m open; (3) 15m MSS candle range midpoint retest with 5m directional body/close confirmation, enter next 5m open. Retest expires after 12 5m bars; invalidation before entry rejects the event.
- Variations: external and local pivot strengths 2/3; MSS wait 4/8; three entry modes; stop buffer 0/0.1 x ATR14(5m) known at MSS; targets 1.5R/2R/3R; no filter vs completed 4H EMA50/200 alignment; both/long-only/short-only; all hours vs 07:00–19:00 UTC. Total 1,728 configurations.
- Execution: next 5m open; 4h maximum holding period; one position at a time per configuration; same-bar stop before target; adverse stop gaps filled at worse open; target gaps filled at target; timeout at final close. Flat round-trip cost sensitivity 6/12/20bp. Funding excluded because settlement-time attribution is not yet independently verified.
- Chronological split: 70/15/15 by 5m row index. No trade may cross its sample boundary. OOS is evaluated only for one candidate passing both gates.
- Gates: training >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, positive active months >=50%, max DD >=-15R. Validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, positive active months >=50%, max DD >=-10R. OOS only after a validation pass; choose one candidate using validation PF, monthly stability and drawdown, not OOS.
- Outputs: matrix, 6/12/20bp cost sensitivity for training-eligible candidates, selected trades at 12bp, selected monthly/regime breakdown, metadata and input hash.
- Timestamp-convention audit: the older 15m sweep engine keeps raw 15m candle-open timestamps in `d.index`, while derived 1h bars are right-labelled at close. `searchsorted(side="left")` therefore maps the 1h confirmation close to the 15m candle opening at that time—the first 15m candle after confirmation—and is causal. The new 5m engine derives right-labelled 15m close timestamps, so it correctly uses `side="right"` to skip the candle ending at confirmation. These are consistent conventions; no older-engine lookahead defect is established.
- Deterministic tests cover strict pivot availability, FVG formation only after the third candle is fully closed, same-bar stop-first ordering and adverse stop gaps.
- Code commits: `6b7a874536f08e0e3a5ff1359720ffaef448ac07` (initial engine), `d19ba1fead823db3d7cfd67fbbea7b0fa9112bd3` (FVG test fixture/cost-row fix), `31cf6d4f2ae599a7e78dd6b8ac789a68fdba22e1` (retain simultaneous 1H high and low pivots), `72a5ac98d58dbeb1c323b1daaf93ff81f7fe420b` (workflow correction).
- Status at ledger update: dedicated Actions run `43288558230` was in progress at setup/dependency completion and entering deterministic tests; no historical download or strategy performance results verified yet. A superseded earlier run was cancelled. Do not report profitability until artifacts and job logs are inspected.

- Follow-up code audit before accepting results: ATR true range now resets at each 5m data-gap segment (the first bar after a gap uses only its own high-low, not the prior segment's close). Completed 4H context is masked when its last close is 4h old or older. Added a deterministic gap-reset regression test. Commit: `fb3728bef9cc7b4b0ecf404de39a0b8556ac74e0`. The earlier in-progress run used the pre-fix code and must not be used for results; the corrected workflow run must complete and its artifact be inspected.

## BTC/ETH rolling residual pair mean-reversion — preregistered (2026-11-12/13)

- Motivation: after rejecting repeated single-asset mechanical trend, breakout and sweep variants, test a distinct market-neutral BTC/ETH relative-value hypothesis rather than adding more filters to a failed signal family.
- Code: `research/btc_eth_pairs_mean_reversion_backtest.py`; dedicated workflow: `.github/workflows/btc-eth-pairs-mean-reversion.yml`; branch `ai-quant-rebuild`. An opt-in `btc-eth-pairs-research` job was also added to the existing branch CI workflow `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, gated by commit message prefix `RUN_BTC_ETH_PAIR_RESEARCH:`, so the study can run without changing the default branch.
- Inputs: two years of Delta Exchange India BTCUSD and ETHUSD traded-price 15m OHLCV downloaded with the repository's resumable `bootstrap_historical_data.py`; only exact common timestamps are used. Any gap starts a new segment; rolling features and trade holding windows cannot cross segments. Input hashes, row counts, gap counts and coverage are retained.
- Hypothesis: a rolling residual between log ETH and log BTC, with positive rolling OLS hedge ratio, may mean-revert after a completed 15m z-score excursion. This is a statistical relative-value hypothesis, not a claim of cointegration or stationarity.
- Causal features: rolling OLS beta/intercept estimated from the prior 10d/30d only; residual mean/std estimated from prior 3d/7d only; current completed 15m close determines z-score; entry is at next 15m open for both legs. Hedge ratio constrained to 0.25-4.0. Optional 30d return-correlation floor 0.6. Completed 1H BTC EMA50/EMA200 regime is retained for attribution only.
- Matrix: beta lookback 960/2,880 bars; residual z lookback 288/672; entry z 1.5/2.0/2.5; stop z 3.5/4.0; target z 0/0.5; max hold 96/192 bars (24h/48h); both/long-spread-only/short-spread-only; no correlation filter / 0.6 correlation floor. Total 576 configurations.
- Execution: freeze signal-time alpha/beta/residual mean/std; synthesize log-spread OHLC using adverse cross-leg extremes; stop checked before target; adverse stop gaps at observed open; target at target; time exit at final close; one pair position at a time/config. No overlapping positions. No trade may cross chronological split or a data gap.
- Costs: 6/12/20bp assumed round-trip per leg, 12bp per leg primary; pair cost is charged against combined gross notional (1 ETH leg + beta BTC leg). Return percentage is reported per gross pair notional and compounded sequentially; net R divides pair P&L by the fixed residual-spread stop distance. Funding, borrow, separate spread and market impact are excluded and must be verified before any promotion.
- Splits/gates: chronological 70/15/15 on aligned 15m rows; train >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, positive active months >=50%, DD>=-20R; validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, positive active months >=50%, DD>=-12R. OOS only after one candidate passes both gates.
- Deterministic tests cover synthetic spread OHLC, same-bar stop-first, adverse stop gaps, future-data perturbation invariance for beta/z features, and gap-segment reset.
- Code commits: `b4078941a95e10ffc3e9a56b469d8fdc500eefdd` (initial code), `ae4c710ac1c2c580aff66e696ca7b3cae8bded20` (pair return and regime reporting), `a48a6dcc16adda4bf078c62f5eb526cd824918af` (signal close timestamp audit), `32319042a63a90b3a719670299811da508f7c2d7` (opt-in CI job and shell continuation correction), `ea7fc028b6eef9b1a78f53ca55b1ff72a144e408` (timestamp convention metadata and trigger).
- Status at preregistration: dedicated workflow [43327866577](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43327866577) had passed dependency installation, compilation and all 7 deterministic pair tests; two-year BTC/ETH 15m data download was in progress. No market backtest result is claimed until the full matrix, metadata and artifact are inspected.
- Known limitations: a rolling regression residual can appear mean-reverting without a stable cointegrating relationship; this is not an ADF/Engle-Granger cointegration proof. Pair fills are synthetic because exact simultaneous intrabar leg execution cannot be reconstructed from OHLC. Funding and borrow are material for 24h/48h positions and are excluded; results are diagnostic only even if the gross/fee-only gates pass.


## BTC/ETH rolling-residual pair mean reversion — execution bug and correction (2026-11-13)

- Initial two-year BTC/ETH 15m data download completed successfully in GitHub Actions run `43327993388`; deterministic pair tests passed. The 576-configuration matrix did NOT execute to completion.
- Exact runtime failure: `AttributeError: 'function' object has no attribute 'to_numpy'` at `make_signals()`, line using `f.corr.to_numpy(float)`. In pandas, `DataFrame.corr` resolves to the method, not the column. Corrected to explicit column access `f["corr"].to_numpy(float)`.
- Added a regression test that exercises signal generation with a DataFrame containing a `corr` column and verifies both correlation-filtered and unfiltered crossing signals.
- Correction commit: `b95f393b1998e49697d3ac04d4b803198c758f5a`; push triggers the dedicated pair workflow. Await successful rerun and artifact verification before reporting any market-performance result.
- Preregistered hypothesis and rules unchanged: rolling OLS of log ETH on log BTC using only prior 10d/30d data; lagged 3d/7d residual mean/std; 15m residual z-score entry 1.5/2.0/2.5; stop z 3.5/4.0; target z 0/0.5; max hold 24h/48h; optional 0.6 correlation filter; both directions or one-sided; next 15m open for both legs; stop-first same-bar synthetic spread OHLC; 6/12/20bp assumed round-trip cost per leg.
- Chronological split 70/15/15 by aligned BTC/ETH 15m observations. Training gate: >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, positive months>=50%, DD>=-20R. Validation gate: >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, positive months>=50%, DD>=-12R. OOS only after both gates.
- Dataset: two years BTC and ETH traded 15m candles downloaded by the failed run; exact row counts, gaps, SHA-256 and performance statistics are to be read from the successful rerun's metadata/artifact, not inferred from the failed run.
- Known limitations remain: rolling residual mean reversion is not proof of cointegration/stationarity; synthetic spread OHLC cannot reconstruct exact intrabar leg ordering; funding, borrow, spread and market impact are excluded; actual fee tiers are unverified. No live or production use.


## BTC/ETH — rolling residual pair mean reversion (2026-11-13)

- Workflow: [run 43357538018](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43357538018); [artifact 14007525650](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43357538018/artifacts/14007525650).
- Code: `research/btc_eth_pairs_mean_reversion_backtest.py`; workflow: `.github/workflows/btc-eth-pairs-mean-reversion.yml`.
- Dataset: 70,127 aligned BTCUSD/ETHUSD 15m candles, 2024-11-12 15:15 UTC to 2026-11-13 02:45 UTC; zero missing intervals in either input or aligned series. Zero-volume rows: BTC 80, ETH 85. CSV SHA-256: BTC `f31d3c0b9ddb13a1355ea78d569af7971f8517b4c2e5812ff82bd96ae9775e1e`; ETH `cb6e4afc818309f3b46608f5b3c055d02a3242625a60a37c63eeb35dfa7bc459`.
- Rules: rolling OLS of log(ETH) on log(BTC), beta lookback 10d/30d, residual mean/std lookback 3d/7d; entry on completed 15m residual z crossing +/-1.5/2.0/2.5; stop z 3.5/4.0; target z 0/0.5; max hold 24h/48h; both directions or one side; correlation filter 0/0.6. Positive beta constrained to 0.25–4.0. Both legs enter at next 15m open with signal-time alpha/beta/mean/std frozen.
- Execution: conservative synthetic log-spread OHLC using adverse cross-leg extremes; stop checked before target; adverse stop gaps at observed open; target fills at target; timeout at final close; one pair position per configuration; no trade crosses split or data gap.
- Costs: primary 12bp assumed round-trip per leg; tested grid includes 6/12/20bp per leg, but cost sensitivity file is empty because no configuration passed the training gate. Funding, borrow, separate spread and market impact excluded.
- Chronological split: 70/15/15 by aligned 15m row index; train end 2026-04-07 23:15 UTC; validation end 2026-07-26 13:00 UTC. 576 configurations; zero training-eligible candidates; zero validation passes; OOS not evaluated.
- Best training-net configuration: beta 30d, residual window 7d, entry z 1.5, stop z 4.0, target z 0.5, 48h max hold, short-spread only, no correlation filter. Train: 155 trades, win rate 72.90%, PF 0.904, net -4.26R, max DD -6.80R, losing streak 4, positive months 35.3%. Validation: 41 trades, win 70.73%, PF 1.066, net +0.70R, max DD -6.36R, losing streak 4, positive months 50%.
- Best validation-only configuration (not selected for OOS): beta 10d, residual window 7d, entry z 1.5, stop z 4.0, target z 0.5, 48h max hold, long-spread only, no correlation filter. Train: 163 trades, win 61.35%, PF 0.586, net -27.06R, max DD -29.66R, losing streak 7, positive months 29.4%. Validation: 49 trades, win 83.67%, PF 1.626, net +5.67R, max DD -2.77R, losing streak 2, positive months 100% (four validation months). This is a clear train/validation regime reversal, not a robust edge.
- Decision: reject. No OOS evaluation. The validation winner's training loss and the best training candidate's weak PF/low monthly consistency fail the preregistered gates.
- Workflow nuance: historical downloads, all 576 configurations, matrix and diagnostic artifacts completed. The final job-summary step failed only because it attempted to read an intentionally empty `cost_sensitivity.csv` with pandas, raising `EmptyDataError`; artifact upload succeeded. A guard for empty CSVs was committed at `8618726295127fe42773a6adb618bacdd34e80e0`; this workflow-file change triggers a clean reproducibility rerun.
- Limitations: residual mean reversion is not proof of cointegration/stationarity; funding/borrow and exact account fees are not verified; synthetic spread OHLC cannot reconstruct exact intrabar ordering of two independent legs.


## BTCUSD — 1H/15m EMA51 pullback-break trigger (2026-11-13)

- Report: `quant_discovery/BTC_EMA51_BREAK_TRIGGER_2026-11-13.md`.
- Code: `research/btc_ema51_break_trigger_intraday_backtest.py`; workflow: `.github/workflows/btc-ema51-break-trigger.yml`; GitHub Actions rerun: [43386574210](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43386574210).

## Next preregistered hypothesis — BTC VWAP trend continuation (planned 2026-11-13)

This is intentionally distinct from the existing `vwap_reversion_intraday_backtest.py`, which tests deviation-band mean reversion. No results exist yet.

- Market/data: BTCUSD traded 5m execution candles with completed 15m and 1H context; two-year history; preserve gaps and segment features.
- Trend regime: long only when completed 1H close > EMA50 and EMA50 > EMA200; short only for the inverse. Optional four-hour EMA50 slope confirmation.
- VWAP context: UTC-day cumulative typical-price VWAP using exchange candle volume; signal uses only completed 15m bars. Require VWAP slope over four completed 15m bars to agree with direction and 15m close on the trend side of VWAP.
- 5m trigger: price pulls back into a 0.10/0.20 ATR band around the current completed 15m VWAP, then a completed 5m candle closes back on the trend side; enter next 5m open. Compare reclaim-close confirmation with break of the prior 5m candle extreme.
- Relative-volume filters: none, 1.0x or 1.5x the prior 20-bar 5m median; never include the current bar in the baseline.
- Stops: signal-candle extreme, 1.0 ATR or 1.5 ATR; targets 1.5R/2R/3R; maximum hold 4h; one position at a time.
- Sessions: all hours, 07:00–13:00 UTC, 13:00–19:00 UTC.
- Costs: 6/12/20bp assumed round trip; exact account fee tier, funding settlement cashflows and spread/market impact must be separately reconciled before any promotion.
- Split/selection: chronological 70/15/15; minimum 40 train and 20 validation trades; PF >=1.05, positive net R, >=50% positive months, DD no worse than -20R train/-12R validation; OOS only after both gates. All feature and holding windows must stay inside contiguous data segments and split boundaries.
- Execution: next 5m open; same-bar stop before target; adverse stop gap at worse open; target at target; no future higher-timeframe candle leakage.
- This is a preregistered research plan only; do not describe it as executed or profitable until the workflow and artifact are independently verified.



### BTC VWAP trend-continuation implementation — 2026-11-13

- Implemented the previously preregistered continuation hypothesis as a separate study; this is not a modification of the older UTC-anchored VWAP reversion experiment.
- Code: \`research/btc_vwap_trend_continuation_backtest.py\`; workflow: \`.github/workflows/btc-vwap-trend-continuation.yml\`.
- Market/data: BTCUSD Delta traded-price 5m OHLCV; workflow downloads two years via \`bootstrap_historical_data.py\`; derives only complete 15m, 1h and 4h bars from contiguous 5m segments.
- Signal rules: completed 1h close > EMA50 > EMA200 for longs, inverse for shorts; completed 15m UTC-day anchored typical-price VWAP slope over four bars must agree and completed 15m close must be on the trend side; completed 5m candle must intersect a 0.10/0.20 ATR5 band around latest completed 15m VWAP and confirm either a close reclaim or close beyond prior 5m extreme; entry at next 5m open.
- Filters: current completed 5m volume compared with median of prior 20 completed 5m bars (none, 1.0x, 1.5x); optional completed 4h EMA50 slope over three completed 4h bars; sessions all UTC, 07:00–13:00 UTC, 13:00–19:00 UTC.
- Risk: signal-candle extreme or 1.0/1.5 ATR5 stop; 1.5R/2R/3R target; maximum 4h hold; one position per configuration; next-open entry; same-bar stop before target; adverse stop-gap fill at worse open; invalid if next open is through stop.
- Matrix: 2 VWAP bands x 2 confirmations x 3 volume filters x 3 stop types x 3 targets x 3 sessions x 2 4h filters = 648 configurations.
- Costs: 6/12/20bp assumed round trip on one BTC leg; primary matrix 12bp. Exact account fee tier, separate spread/market impact and funding remain unverified/excluded. Delta candle volume is exchange-specific, not consolidated market volume.
- Split/gates: chronological 70/15/15 by 5m row count; train >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, positive net R, max DD >=-20R and >=50% positive active months; shortlist top 25 by net R / absolute drawdown; validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, positive net R, max DD >=-12R and >=50% positive active months; one OOS candidate only if both gates pass.
- Causality controls: 5m trigger uses completed signal candle; 15m/1h/4h contexts are as-of joined only at completed-bar availability time; rolling volume median shifted one 5m bar; all rolling features reset at data gaps; incomplete resampled bars excluded; UTC VWAP resets at the UTC-day boundary. A further guard rejects reclaim comparisons across the VWAP UTC-day reset.
- Deterministic self-tests cover same-bar stop priority, adverse stop gaps, next-bar context availability, shifted volume baseline and a synthetic valid trend/VWAP trigger.
- Code commits: engine \`daa179ebacf1272cbaa0ce7e86fa33599fea1d2e\`; candidate-key hardening \`10b999276d3118b502f684760dd97c75b5088dfe\`; gate-indentation correction \`debcf1aac024cefca0c28ffccb5732bc358fcda1\`; workflow expression fix \`29ae52ac0eea1efe95635f99fb00ea2d436d67a7\`; VWAP day-reset guard \`9d4e20d8635690349b3a66adfe6c6ac9b24b6442\`.
- Workflow run \`43415410715\` at commit \`29ae52a\` passed compilation and the initial deterministic tests, then began downloading the two-year 5m BTC dataset. A new run \`43415470933\` was queued for commit \`9d4e20d\` after the UTC-day reset guard; final matrix results and artifact are pending independent verification.
- No performance result is available yet. Do not report this experiment as completed or profitable until the latest run's steps, summary and artifact are inspected.

## BTCUSD — Supertrend stop-management sensitivity (preregistered 2026-11-13)

- Motivation: the existing 15m Supertrend intraday continuation study was completed and rejected (zero validation passes). Rather than repeat its indicator-source/session matrix, this follow-up isolates the unresolved risk-management question: whether the fixed signal-bar Supertrend stop is the main weakness, and whether a causal trailing Supertrend, ATR or recent-swing stop changes the result.
- Reuses research/supertrend_intraday_backtest.py only for its existing prepare_base and TradingView-style build_supertrend helpers. New engine: research/supertrend_stop_management_backtest.py; workflow: .github/workflows/supertrend-stop-management-backtest.yml; branch ai-quant-rebuild.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD traded-price 15m OHLC; fetches up to 730 days. Data is divided into contiguous 15m segments; prepare_base and Supertrend are recomputed independently per segment. 15m EMA50 and completed-1h EMA50/EMA200 must not carry across gaps. Incomplete 1h bars are excluded. SHA-256, rows, gap count, segment count and UTC coverage are retained.
- Signal: completed 15m Supertrend direction flip using source HL2 or 15m EMA50; Wilder RMA ATR length 7/10/14; multiplier 2.0/2.5/3.0; enter next 15m open. Optional completed-1h EMA50 direction filter. Both/long-only/short-only and all-hours or 07:00–19:00 UTC.
- Stop methods: (1) fixed signal-bar Supertrend line; (2) trailing Supertrend line ratcheted only in the favourable direction and updated from the previous completed 15m bar; (3) fixed 1.0 ATR from entry; (4) fixed 1.5 ATR from entry; (5) prior-five-bar swing extreme with 0.1 ATR buffer. Initial risk is fixed at entry; target is 1.5R/2R/3R; max hold 32 bars (8h); one position per configuration.
- Matrix: 2 sources x 3 ATR lengths x 3 multipliers x 5 stop methods x 3 targets x 2 HTF filters x 3 direction modes x 2 session modes = 3,240 configurations.
- Execution: next 15m open; invalid if initial stop is on the wrong side or risk exceeds 5% of entry; trailing stop updates use only the previous completed bar; stop checked before target on same-bar ambiguity; adverse stop gaps fill at worse open; target at target; time exit at final 15m close; no trade may cross a data gap or split boundary.
- Costs: primary assumed 12bp round trip on one BTC leg; fixed-trade sensitivity recomputed at 6/12/20bp. Account-specific fee tier, spread and market impact remain unverified/excluded. Funding is not modelled for the 8h maximum hold.
- Splits/gates: chronological 70/15/15 by row index; training >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, DD>=-25R and >=50% positive active months; shortlist top 25 by net R/drawdown; validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, net R>0, DD>=-15R and >=50% positive active months. OOS only if a candidate passes both gates.
- Diagnostics: best training and best validation candidates retain trade-level records, monthly summaries, bull/bear/range-or-warmup attribution, losing streak and cost sensitivity. Validation-only candidate is never promoted if its training counterpart fails.
- Deterministic tests: gap segmentation; first post-gap true range uses only that candle's high-low; 15m EMA and 1h EMA do not carry stale state across gaps; Supertrend direction resets at segment start; trailing stop only ratchets favourably; same-bar stop priority and adverse gap-through fills.
- Initial commit: d5af5784fe46716b6e6a637e81c5c26092b80b74; workflow run https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43450588548. At preregistration, dependency installation was in progress; no data or performance result is claimed yet.
- Prior Supertrend evidence remains relevant: the earlier intraday 1,080-config study https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/42823967966 had zero validation passes; the older multi-day OOS study https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/42781648124 had negative aggregate OOS results. This stop-management study is a bounded follow-up, not a reason to re-open those rejected candidates.
- No production rules, main branch, live orders or execution alerts are changed.

## BTCUSD 15m support/resistance rejection — preregistered implementation (2026-11-14; results pending)

- Motivation: follow the existing research roadmap for a structurally distinct prior-day/4H support-resistance rejection model after CRT, channel failure, SMC/MSS, EMA pullback, VWAP continuation and Supertrend variants failed their train/validation gates. This is not a duplicate of the Donchian failed-breakout study: levels are prior complete UTC-day extremes or confirmed 4H swing pivots.
- Code: research/support_resistance_rejection_backtest.py; workflow .github/workflows/support-resistance-rejection.yml; branch ai-quant-rebuild.
- Data: BTCUSD Delta 15m traded-price OHLC via the repository's existing crt_backtest.fetch("15m"); fetcher excludes the current incomplete candle, deduplicates and rejects malformed OHLC. Gaps create new segments and are never filled. Dataset hash/date coverage and 70/15/15 boundaries are written to metadata after execution.
- Level definitions: (1) previous UTC-day high/low from a day with exactly 96 contiguous 15m candles, available only during the immediately following UTC day and only if the current bars remain in the same contiguous data segment; (2) 4H swing high/low with two completed 4H bars on each side, exposed only at the close of the second right-side confirmation bar and masked across raw data-segment gaps. 1H EMA50/200 context uses only complete 1H bars and resets after gaps/incomplete-hour segments. Wilder ATR14 resets per 15m segment.
- Signal: 15m resistance rejection/short or support rejection/long using either sweep_reclaim (wick crosses level by 0/0.1 ATR and close returns across it) or near_reject (wick is within 0/0.1 ATR of the level and closes back on the rejection side). Entry is next 15m open after the rejection close, or after an optional next-bar close through the rejection candle's opposite extreme and then the following 15m open.
- Risk/exit: stop beyond rejection candle wick plus 0/0.1 ATR14; fixed 1.5R/2R/3R target; 32-bar/8h max hold; one position per configuration; reject initial risk <=0 or >5% of entry; same-bar stop first; adverse stop gap at worse open; target at target; time exit at final close; no trade may cross a data gap or split boundary.
- Matrix: 2 level sources x 2 approach modes x 2 ATR tolerances x 2 confirmation modes x 2 stop buffers x 3 targets x 2 HTF filters x 3 direction modes x 2 sessions = 1,152 configurations.
- Costs/splits: assumed 12bp round trip on one BTC leg; sensitivity 6/12/20bp; actual fee tier, spread/market impact and funding are not verified/modelled. Chronological 70/15/15. Train >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, positive net R, max DD >=-25R and >=50% positive active months; top 25 by train net-R/abs(DD); validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, positive net R, max DD >=-15R and >=50% positive active months. OOS only if both gates pass.
- Regression tests cover next-day level availability, incomplete prior-day invalidation, 4H pivot right-side confirmation timestamp, pivot masking across a data gap, immediate rejection entry, next-bar confirmation and failed confirmation, same-bar stop priority and adverse gap fills. The final fixture correction is in commit 6459f6b6cbb0ac41bea7b1690dd8a58a2d247f6c; earlier causal-level hardening is 9d289b2e1c0fb85242b8ca9f3b8457ba911e2073; test expansion is c9baa3fc6e1ff7ee5c18ee5b8134bc3442129a26; initial engine is 17c6b83d48703edeadc3dae2cbad748fbddc78f8.
- Workflow added and expression/shell quoting corrected in commit d4e446b859d3fd139ab15154768507af661236bd. The latest GitHub Actions run ID and execution status must be checked before reporting any performance result. This is preregistered research only, not a validated or live strategy.


### Support/resistance implementation verification notes — 2026-11-14

- First two dedicated runs failed only in deterministic test fixtures: one expected the 4H pivot at the wrong 15m index; the next short-target fixture did not actually touch its target. Both expectations were corrected.
- The successful self-test run then exposed a code-path NameError during matrix execution: a stale reference to the removed variable "aligned" remained in the optional EMA regime filter. Removed the stale reference and the now-unused EMA arrays in commit c008379e74518c472d904615ea014a75826d77e9.
- During this audit, a separate causal-level persistence issue was found and corrected in commit 34fcf5f0b8bf0676336a81e88b6f15a6714224e7: a newly confirmed 4H pivot high previously erased the last active support (and vice versa). The pivot builder now carries forward the most recent high and low independently, resets both on a new complete-4H segment, and masks them across raw data gaps. A deterministic regression test verifies that a later pivot low does not erase the earlier confirmed high.
- Latest run [43491328896](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43491328896) uses code commit c008379e74518c472d904615ea014a75826d77e9. Compilation and all 13 deterministic tests passed; the 1,152-configuration market-data matrix is still executing at the time of this note. No market-performance result is available yet. Prior failed runs are not strategy results.

## BTCUSD 15m volatility-regime-conditioned failed Donchian breakout reversal — preregistered (2026-11-14)

- Motivation: continue the volatility-regime-conditioned reversal roadmap after support/resistance rejection produced no train-qualified configurations. This reuses the existing failed-Donchian-breakout reversal hypothesis, but explicitly tests whether failure behaviour differs across low/medium/high realized-volatility states; it is not a new independent signal family and must be interpreted as a conditional diagnostic.
- Code: `research/failed_breakout_reversal_intraday_backtest.py`; workflow `.github/workflows/failed-breakout-reversal.yml`; branch `ai-quant-rebuild`. Code commit `9725b7875dad64e0aa44f5e843fe3f105d506c82`; workflow/test integration commit `174aacfe227ac8948755777215518a5a7e96f33f`.
- Prior code audit found the existing 1H context used forward-filled hourly values without masking the source segment. Corrected it: complete 1H bars retain their source 15m segment; close/EMA context is masked until a complete hour from the current contiguous segment is available. This prevents stale pre-gap context from leaking after a data discontinuity.
- Volatility regime: ATR14/close compared with prior 30-day rolling 33rd/67th percentiles (2,880 15m bars; minimum 720 prior observations), both quantiles shifted one bar and recomputed independently per contiguous data segment. Current bar cannot affect its own threshold. Regime filters: all, low, mid, high.
- Base signal remains a causal failed Donchian breakout: prior shifted 20/55-bar channel close breakout; close returns inside the same frozen boundary within 1/3 bars; optional opposite-body confirmation; entry next 15m open in reversal direction; stop beyond full breakout excursion plus 0/0.1 ATR; 1.5R/2R/3R/4R target; 8h maximum hold; optional completed 1H EMA50/200 alignment; both/long-only/short-only; all or 07-19 UTC session.
- Matrix: original 768 configurations x 4 volatility-regime filters = 3,072 configurations. Primary assumed cost 12bp round trip; 6/12/20bp sensitivity. Costs are assumptions; no funding or separate spread/market impact model. Fixed UTC session proxy; DST ignored.
- Execution: stop-first OHLC ambiguity, adverse stop-gap fill at worse open, no trade crosses a data gap or chronological split. Added centralized exit-fill function and eight deterministic tests covering long/short same-bar stop priority, adverse gaps, target fills, HTF reset after a synthetic data gap, volatility warm-up after gaps, and future-perturbation invariance of past volatility labels.
- Chronological split 70/15/15; training >=40 trades, positive net R, PF>=1.05, max DD >=-25R; top 25 by train net_R/(abs(DD)+1); validation >=20 trades, positive net R, PF>=1.05, max DD >=-15R and >=50% positive active months; OOS only after both gates.
- Current execution: dedicated workflow run [43525783981](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43525783981) uses the updated workflow with self-tests and 3,072 configurations; status must be checked before claiming tests or market results completed. A superseded run [43525770920](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43525770920) started from the code commit with the old workflow (768 configurations, no self-test step); do not treat it as the final study if the updated run succeeds.
- No performance result is claimed at preregistration. This remains experimental and is not a live strategy.

## BTCUSD 15m regular RSI divergence + neckline break — preregistered (2026-11-14)

- Motivation: test a distinct swing-reversal hypothesis after mechanical support/resistance and volatility-conditioned failed-breakout variants failed their selection gates. This is not the previously tested RSI extreme-reclaim system: it requires regular divergence between two confirmed price pivots and a subsequent neckline break.
- Code: research/rsi_divergence_neckline_backtest.py; workflow .github/workflows/rsi-divergence-neckline.yml; branch ai-quant-rebuild. Initial code commit dcfa50929eb25cafed9e579603fb17a918dfded8; RSI warm-up/monthly-metric correction commit 86f0904dd1528c3af94b02067b47e5cf3bfc7531; workflow commit 0327bb19fa2f39ca780d783540d421a279d941fd.
- Signal: on 15m, identify a confirmed pivot low/high using left/right strengths 2/2, 3/3 or 5/5. A pivot becomes available only after all right-side bars close. Bullish regular divergence: current confirmed pivot low is lower than the prior confirmed pivot low while RSI at the current pivot is higher; bearish regular divergence is symmetric. Neckline is the intervening high between pivot lows or low between pivot highs. Require a subsequent 15m close to cross the neckline within 3 or 6 bars after pivot confirmation; enter at the following 15m open. A breakout that occurred before pivot confirmation is not accepted.
- Variations: RSI length 7/14; pivot strengths 2/2, 3/3, 5/5; neckline-break window 3/6; stop buffer 0/0.1 x ATR14 beyond the divergence pivot extreme; targets 1.5R/2R/3R; optional completed 1h EMA50/EMA200 directional regime; both/long-only/short-only; all hours or 07–19 UTC. Matrix: 864 configurations.
- Execution: max 8h/32 bars; one position/config; same-bar stop first; adverse stop gap fills at worse open; target gap receives no favourable improvement; time exit at final 15m close; no trade crosses data gaps or chronological sample boundaries. Both divergence pivots must occur inside the relevant sample to prevent a validation/OOS trade from depending on a prior-sample pivot.
- Data: BTCUSD Delta 15m via existing crt_backtest.fetch("15m"); normalized OHLC, deduplicated, invalid bars removed, contiguous segments retained without gap filling. Hash/date coverage and 70/15/15 boundaries are written to metadata by the workflow. Only complete 1h candles built from four same-segment 15m bars feed EMA50/EMA200 context; context and RSI/ATR warm-up reset after gaps.
- Costs/splits: assumed 12bp round trip per BTC leg; 6/12/20bp sensitivity; no funding or separate spread/market-impact model; actual fee tier unverified. Chronological 70/15/15. Train gate >=40 trades, PF>=1.05, positive net R, max DD >=-25R and >=50% positive active months; top 25 by net_R/(abs(DD)+1); validation >=20 trades, PF>=1.05, positive net R, max DD >=-15R and >=50% positive active months; OOS only after both.
- Deterministic tests cover long/short same-bar stop priority, adverse stop gaps, target fills, confirmed pivot timing, gap-reset of ATR/RSI/hourly context, finite RSI warm-up and future-perturbation invariance of prior indicators. A code audit corrected the first RSI delta warm-up and monthly aggregation before relying on the new run.
- Performance results are pending the latest GitHub Actions run. This is preregistered research only; do not claim profitability or OOS results until the workflow completes and the artifact is inspected.

## Next crypto research direction — BTC/ETH cross-sectional momentum rotation (preregistered concept; not yet executed)

- Motivation: the BTC/ETH rolling-residual mean-reversion experiment was rejected. Test the opposite hypothesis—relative momentum persistence—without reusing the residual z-score signal. This is a two-asset research universe only and cannot support broad-universe survivorship claims.
- Proposed rule: on completed 15m bars, calculate trailing 24h/72h/168h BTC and ETH returns; at scheduled 4h/8h/24h decision times, compare the two assets. Long the stronger asset and short the weaker only when the relative-return spread exceeds a preregistered threshold; compare market-neutral two-leg and long-strong-only variants. Entry is next 15m open; rebalance only at scheduled decision times; hold until next rebalance or fixed 4h/8h/24h horizon.
- Planned controls: signal lag at decision close, no use of current incomplete bars, equal-dollar notional per leg, per-leg 6/12/20bp round-trip cost sensitivity, no overlapping positions beyond the intended rebalance schedule, chronological 70/15/15 split, conservative stop/exit if any stop variants are introduced, and separate BTC/ETH attribution. Funding/borrow remains excluded until verified event-time settlement attribution is available.
- This is a concept only. Do not treat as a completed or profitable experiment; exact threshold grid, cost accounting, and data checks must be finalized before implementation.

## Research methodology reset (2026-11-14)

- User challenged the repeated rejected models and asked us to improve the research process or monetize signals differently. We audited the repository code and ledger rather than assuming every underlying idea is unprofitable.
- Audit found methodological limitations: short 70/15/15 validation/OOS windows (~3.6 months each on a two-year sample); weak evidence from 20-trade validation minimums; large parameter grids and winner's-curse risk; fixed RR/holding horizons that conflate signal quality with exit quality; stop-first OHLC handling without reporting ambiguity bounds; coarse assumed round-trip bps rather than maker/taker, spread, slippage and funding attribution; incomplete walk-forward fold, uncertainty, exposure and account-equity reporting; and risk-unit R metrics that are not account returns.
- Important distinction: these are limitations, not proof that prior results are all wrong. Prior studies remain historical exploratory tests and are not retroactively reclassified.
- Effective protocol: pause giant grids; preregister one economic hypothesis with a small interpretable variant set; compare against same-period flat, buy/hold and simple directional benchmarks; use rolling/expanding chronological walk-forward folds plus one final untouched holdout; separate entry signal forward-return diagnostics from exit/position-management tests; use 1m/finer execution data where available; otherwise report pessimistic stop-first and optimistic target-first bounds plus ambiguous-bar counts; model entry/exit costs separately and funding only at verified settlement timestamps; report fold results, confidence/bootstrap intervals, exposure, turnover, R and equity drawdown; never use validation-only leaderboard rows for selection.
- Next hypotheses under this protocol: (1) fixed-session range sweep and re-entry targeting VWAP/opposite range, (2) HTF trend as directional bias with 15m pullback measured first as a forward-return event study, (3) volatility compression-to-expansion continuation. Start with BTC and the finest reliable execution data; do not assume 1m data exists until verified.
- New protocol document: `research/RESEARCH_METHOD_RESET_2026-11-14.md`, commit `03a215a444fe1b94a6a0273481751107f560cc7b`.
- Scope remains research-only on `ai-quant-rebuild`; no `main` or production changes, no orders, no execution alerts.


## 2026-11-14 — Preregistered BTC session-range sweep event study (implementation)

- Hypothesis frozen before evaluation: define UTC 00:00–06:00 range; during 06:00–18:00 UTC, first 5m candle that sweeps one range edge and closes back inside creates a directional event; enter at next 5m open; at most one event per UTC day; exclude bars sweeping both edges.
- First phase measures directional forward returns at 15m, 1h, 4h and 8h. It does not optimize stops/targets or claim executable PnL.
- Cost sensitivity: 6/12/20 bps round trip, explicitly assumptions. Data source: public Delta BTCUSD traded-price 5m candles. Gaps and coverage are reported.
- Calendar evaluation counts use fixed consecutive 2-month windows after a 6-month initial warm-up; no parameter selection is performed on these windows.
- Limitations: forward-return event study is not yet a matched-control/placebo test; 5m OHLC cannot resolve intrabar execution; cost assumptions are not verified fees/slippage. No promotion decision is possible from this phase alone.
- Status at commit: code/workflow added; backtest has NOT yet been run. Await GitHub Actions evidence.

- Frozen rules: complete UTC 00:00–06:00 Asia range (all 72 5m candles required); during 06:00–18:00 UTC, first candle to sweep one edge and close back inside; entry next 5m open; one event/day; both-edge sweep bars ignored. 662 events (308 long/354 short), each with one deterministic same-day/same-side non-sweep random-time control, excluding +/-30m around the signal. Forward horizons: 15m, 1h, 4h, 8h.

This was corrected before the authoritative run: 6/12/20bp round trip now subtracts 0.06/0.12/0.20 percentage points respectively.
