- Corrected paired results (mean % return; 662 pairs at each horizon):

| Horizon | Event gross | Event net @6bp RT | Event net @12bp RT | Event net @20bp RT | Control net @12bp RT | Event minus control |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 15m | -0.006 | -0.066 | -0.126 | -0.206 | -0.136 | +0.010 |
| 1h | -0.024 | -0.084 | -0.144 | -0.224 | -0.149 | +0.005 |
| 4h | -0.067 | -0.127 | -0.187 | -0.267 | -0.230 | +0.043 |
| 8h | -0.060 | -0.120 | -0.180 | -0.260 | -0.256 | +0.076 |

- At 12bp RT, positive event-month fraction was 0% (15m), 4% (1h), 24% (4h), and 16% (8h). The 8h event-minus-control lift was positive in 8/9 consecutive 2-month evaluation blocks after the initial 6-month warm-up, but the event's absolute mean net return remained negative in 8/9 blocks. This is relative improvement over a crude control, not a profitable strategy.
- This is a forward-return event study, not a stop/target backtest: win-rate, PF, R, stop/target ambiguity and drawdown are not applicable. Costs are simple sensitivity assumptions; no spread, slippage, verified fee tier or funding has been applied. The same-day random-time control is descriptive, not a randomized causal benchmark. No regime stratification or separately sequestered final holdout was run.
- Decision: do not promote or trade this standalone fade. There may be weak relative information at 4h/8h, but the signal does not overcome even the 6bp round-trip sensitivity in absolute mean returns. Next bounded hypothesis: test whether a completed 4h trend-alignment condition improves the sweep event, using a time-of-day-matched control and chronological train/validation/final holdout; do not open a broad parameter grid.

BTC/ETH funding 1h: 17,495 rows each, 99.8174% observed coverage, eight 5-hour gaps per asset at matching timestamps. Missing funding intervals are not filled.

- Funding OHLC values are preserved raw (BTC close range -0.08008859 to 0.07886045; ETH -0.02503412 to 0.04851337). Units, settlement cadence and sign convention remain unverified. Do not apply funding costs to backtests until semantics are independently established.
- Archive is an Actions artifact, not committed market data. It expires 2026-11-28; download/materialize before expiry if the next research run needs the raw files.


## 2026-11-14 — Preregistration: BTC session sweep with completed 4h trend alignment

- Hypothesis: the base Asia-range sweep/re-entry fade may have relative information but negative unconditional returns. A single economically motivated filter is tested: only take the fade when it agrees with a completed 4h trend (long after a low sweep only if 4h close > EMA50 > EMA200; short after a high sweep only if 4h close < EMA50 < EMA200).
- 4h candles are built only from 48 contiguous closed 5m traded-price bars, labelled at their close; the latest completed 4h regime is forward-filled to the entry timestamp. No incomplete 4h bar is used.
- Controls: same UTC day, same side, same 4h regime, random non-sweep bar, excluding +/-30m around the signal; deterministic RNG seed 20261114. Controls are descriptive matched benchmarks, not causal controls.
- Frozen chronological periods: development 2024-11-14 through 2025-11-14; validation 2025-11-14 through 2026-05-14; final period 2026-05-14 through 2026-11-14. No parameter tuning or selection within these periods. The final period is untouched by this filter experiment, though the unfiltered base event study has previously been inspected.
- Report 1h/4h/8h forward returns, 6/12/20bp round-trip cost sensitivities, aligned vs misaligned attribution, counts and monthly/fold stability. This remains an event study, not a stop/target backtest.
- Status: preregistered; not yet executed. Research-only; no production changes or orders.


## 2026-11-14 — BTC Asia sweep with completed 4h trend alignment — completed; no absolute edge

- Authoritative study [43571219538](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43571219538) completed successfully. A fresh reproducibility/robustness rerun [43589097555](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43589097555) also passed, including the new audit script; artifact [14111022682](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43589097555/artifacts/14111022682). Deterministic CI [43589101745](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43589101745) passed.
- Frozen signal: same Asia-range sweep fade as the base study, but only take low-sweep longs when completed 4h close > EMA50 > EMA200 and high-sweep shorts when completed 4h close < EMA50 < EMA200. Completed 4h bars are formed from 48 contiguous closed 5m bars and exposed only at the right-edge close. Primary cost assumption 12bp round trip; sensitivity 6/20bp. Same-day controls match direction and completed 4h regime.
- Independent audit added a stricter same-day, same-direction, same-4h-regime, same-3h-UTC-time-bucket non-sweep control outside +/-30m of the event. It matched 661/661 events (100%); reconstructed 4h regimes had zero mismatches. The fresh 2y BTCUSD 5m sample contained 210,240 bars; the audit classified 191 trend-aligned, 212 countertrend and 258 neutral events. The category split is important: the original "misaligned" bucket combined countertrend and neutral.
- Final six-month holdout, trend-aligned only (n=50), at 12bp round-trip assumption: 1h mean event net -0.175%, time-bucket control -0.216%, paired lift +0.041% (7-observation moving-block 95% CI -0.088% to +0.198%); 4h event -0.129%, control -0.256%, lift +0.127% (CI -0.032% to +0.285%); 8h event -0.098%, control -0.167%, lift +0.069% (CI -0.080% to +0.242%). None of the aligned holdout lift intervals excludes zero, and absolute event returns remain negative.
- Validation aligned results were also negative in absolute terms: 4h mean net -0.361%, lift only +0.007%; 8h mean net -0.308%, lift +0.022%. Development showed a different pattern. Countertrend events were notably weak in the final holdout (4h lift -0.112%, CI -0.346% to +0.073%). Neutral events had a 1h relative lift +0.238% (CI +0.077% to +0.395%) but absolute event net was still -0.051%.
- This remains a forward-return event study, not an execution backtest; PF, R, stop/target ambiguity and trade drawdown are not applicable. The bootstrap is an uncertainty diagnostic, not proof of causality. Costs remain assumptions; spread, slippage, market impact and funding are excluded.
- Decision: reject as a standalone trade rule. The filter may improve relative performance versus a crude matched control in some horizons, but it does not produce a positive absolute net forward return robustly. Do not tune this inspected holdout.

## 2026-11-14 — BTC/ETH 72h cross-sectional relative momentum — completed, rejected

- Code: `research/btc_eth_cross_sectional_momentum_backtest.py`; dedicated workflow [43589236769](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43589236769) completed successfully; artifact [14111766678](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43589236769/artifacts/14111766678). Compile and five deterministic tests passed. Code SHA at run: `fc5618b80c8135ceec891f78117fdacf3c727043`.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD and ETHUSD traded-price 15m OHLCV, 70,127 exact aligned rows from 2024-11-13 23:30 UTC to 2026-11-14 11:00 UTC; zero gaps/irregular intervals; 80 BTC and 85 ETH zero-volume rows retained. Aligned data hash: `84851a089c539bf926418dcb658398565254f1b3744d41b713a0fa93a7d2a1de`.
- Preregistered rule: every 4h UTC boundary, compute 72h return for each asset using only the last completed 15m close and its 288-bar lag. If BTC outperforms ETH by >=1.00 percentage point, equal-dollar 1x long BTC/short ETH; if ETH outperforms by >=1.00pp, reverse. Entry at next 15m open, exit at the close exactly 4h later. Also report a long-only stronger-asset variant and constant relative-direction benchmarks. No parameter grid or tuning. 4,364 decisions; 2,874 active intervals.
- Costs: 6/12/20bp assumed round trip per leg; 12bp primary. Pair returns are per unit of total gross notional, not account equity/margin. Funding, borrow, spread and separate market-impact/slippage are excluded; a 4h hold can cross a funding timestamp.
- Development (1,641 active intervals): pair win 33.70%, PF 0.532, mean net -0.1169% per active interval, compounded -85.67%, max DD -85.79%, max losing streak 15, 0/13 positive months. Long-only stronger asset: win 44.42%, PF 0.822, mean -0.0855%, compounded -78.52%, max DD -80.99%, losing streak 9, 3/13 positive months.
- Validation (628 active intervals): pair win 24.52%, PF 0.264, mean -0.1546%, compounded -62.29%, max DD -62.29%, losing streak 18, 0/7 positive months. Long-only stronger asset: win 42.99%, PF 0.682, mean -0.1511%, compounded -62.92%, max DD -63.06%, losing streak 13, 0/7 positive months.
- Final six-month holdout (604 active intervals): pair win 23.01%, PF 0.310, mean net -0.1286%, compounded -54.17%, max DD -54.24%, losing streak 23, 0/7 positive months. Long-only stronger asset: win 41.39%, PF 0.736, mean -0.1071%, compounded -49.43%, max DD -50.43%, losing streak 12, 2/7 positive months. At only 6bp round trip, holdout pair still averaged -0.0686% (PF 0.520) and stronger-only -0.0471% (PF 0.873). At 20bp, both deteriorated further.
- Six-decision block-bootstrap 95% CI for final holdout mean net return at 12bp: pair -0.1520% to -0.1028%; stronger-only -0.1933% to -0.0193%. Both are negative. R is not applicable because this is a fixed-horizon forward/rotation study without a stop-defined risk unit.
- Decision: reject 72h/4h BTC-ETH relative momentum and its long-only stronger-asset variant. Negative results are consistent across development, validation and holdout and survive even the 6bp cost sensitivity. Do not reverse the signal or tune this inspected holdout; any reversal hypothesis needs a separately preregistered future sample.
- Scope: research-only on `ai-quant-rebuild`; no production rules, main branch, live orders or execution alerts changed.

At 6bp, the 8h event mean was slightly positive (+0.047%), but its matched control was substantially stronger and the paired lift remained negative; at 20bp all three horizons were negative.

It underperformed matched non-event controls in most periods/horizons, especially 4h/8h; a small positive 8h mean at 6bp is not robust and disappears at the primary 12bp assumption. This is a forward-return event study, not a stop/target backtest; PF, R, stop ambiguity and trade drawdown are not applicable. Funding, spread, market impact and verified fee tier remain excluded.

## 2026-11-14 — BTC 1h EMA20 pullback with completed 4h trend bias — implementation

- Hypothesis/rules frozen before results: completed 4h close > EMA50 > EMA200 permits longs only; inverse permits shorts only. On completed 1h candle, long requires previous 1h close <= prior EMA20, current low within +/-0.10 ATR14 of EMA20, current close > EMA20 and > open. Short is the exact inverse using current high. Entry is next 1h open. Global cooldown is 8 hours.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD traded-price 15m OHLCV, two-year archive; aggregate only complete contiguous 4-bar 1h and 16-bar 4h groups. EMA and ATR features reset at data gaps. A 4h regime becomes available only at its right-labelled close.
- Signal event study horizons: 4h, 8h, 24h. Cost sensitivities: 6/12/20 bps round trip (assumptions, not verified fees/slippage). One deterministic non-signal control per event, matched same UTC day, side, completed 4h regime and 3h UTC bucket; no replacement; exclude controls within +/-1 hour of the event.
- Calendar windows frozen: development 2024-11-14 to 2025-11-14; validation 2025-11-14 to 2026-05-14; final holdout 2026-05-14 to 2026-11-14. Any outcome ending after its period end is excluded. No parameter search.
- Diagnostics: trade-like win rate/PF and event-sequence drawdown are reported on fixed-horizon returns, but this is not a stop-defined execution backtest; no R multiple or account-equity drawdown is claimed. 24h outcomes can overlap because the event cooldown is 8h. Block bootstrap is descriptive and matched controls do not establish causality.
- Status at implementation: script/workflow committed; no market result exists until the dedicated GitHub Actions run completes and artifact/report are audited. Research only; no production changes or orders.

## 2026-11-15 — BTC OI contraction + price impulse walk-forward execution study — preregistered, pending run

- New one-rule test to replace the old 72-configuration OI contraction matrix with a frozen, interpretable execution hypothesis and chronological walk-forward evaluation.
- Signal: completed 1h OI close-to-close change <0 and z-score <= -1.5 versus prior-only 168h rolling mean/std (minimum 120 observations), combined with absolute BTC 1h close return >= prior-only 168h 90th percentile. Direction follows the sign of BTC's 1h return. OI candle close is aligned to its availability time (+1h).
- Entry: next 5m open at the completed 1h signal close. Stop: 1.5x Wilder ATR14 of the last completed 15m candle. Target: 2R. Maximum hold and signal cooldown: 8h. One position at a time. Execution on 5m traded-price OHLC; same-bar stop/target ambiguity is counted and stop-first, adverse stop gaps use the worse open, targets fill at target.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD traded-price 5m and BTC OI 1h, two-year request. Complete 15m/1h aggregation only; feature windows reset at gaps and non-positive OI close sentinels. Hashes, gap counts and outlier counts are recorded.
- Evaluation: initial 6-calendar-month train; expanding train with consecutive 2-month validation folds; final untouched 6-calendar-month holdout. Each trade's full 8h maximum horizon must fit inside its evaluation period. No parameter grid or holdout tuning.
- Costs: 6/12/20bp round-trip assumptions; 12bp primary. Funding excluded because the archive retains raw funding units and settlement semantics are not yet sufficiently verified. No verified spread/impact model.
- Metrics: trades, win rate, PF, net R, average R, drawdown, losing streak, ambiguous bars, positive-month fraction, exposure, side/regime/month attribution, and moving-block bootstrap CI where sample size permits.
- This study is distinct from the earlier OI contraction grid. Status at preregistration: code/workflow being added; no market result until the dedicated Actions run completes and artifact is audited.


### BTC OI walk-forward pipeline corrections before market evaluation

- Initial run [43630889532](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43630889532) failed the synthetic resampling assertion. The test was corrected to expect the one complete 15m bucket while confirming the incomplete bucket is dropped.
- Second run [43630921450](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43630921450) passed all 8 causal/execution self-tests and downloaded the full BTC datasets (210,381 5m price rows, one gap; 17,531 OI 1h rows, zero gaps), but the strategy loader rejected a zero OI candle low. The archive audit had already documented zero-low sentinels with positive close values; the strategy uses only OI close. The loader was corrected to allow a zero low only for OI while still requiring positive open/high/close, nonnegative low, and valid OHLC geometry. A regression test now proves the sentinel is accepted only in the OI-specific mode.
- No market performance result was produced by either failed run. A corrected run is triggered by the latest `RUN_OI_WF:` commit; results will be reported only after the dedicated run succeeds.


## 2026-11-15 — BTC OI contraction + price impulse walk-forward execution study — completed, rejected

- Authoritative workflow [43631140063](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43631140063) completed successfully; artifact [14128535494](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43631140063/artifacts/14128535494). All 8 causal/execution self-tests passed on the authoritative run. Earlier pipeline-only failures [43630889532](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43630889532) and [43630921450](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43630921450) were corrected before this run and are not market-performance evidence.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD 5m traded OHLCV, 210,382 rows from 2024-11-14 07:20 UTC to 2026-11-14 19:10 UTC, one 5m gap; 70,126 complete 15m bars and 17,530 complete 1h bars. Price CSV SHA-256 `2d516c278f3aadb1162bd256e9032efe58d9a08478ea4e26256226f03999d734`. Delta BTC OI 1h: 17,531 rows, zero timestamp gaps, zero non-positive close rows, two zero-low sentinels (allowed only for OI; strategy uses close), 293 hourly OI changes >20%. OI CSV SHA-256 `9477b80af3b22f98708021b82fcde20575d6b9a445961fda898c5461e7e8c56d`.
- Frozen signal: completed 1h OI close-to-close change <0 and prior-only 168h z-score <= -1.5 (minimum 120 prior observations), with absolute BTC 1h close return >= prior-only 168h 90th percentile. Direction follows BTC 1h return. OI candle close is treated as available only at its close (+1h). Entry next 5m open. Stop 1.5x Wilder ATR14 of last completed 15m bar; fixed 2R target; max hold 8h; 8h cooldown; one position at a time. No parameter search.
- Execution: 5m traded OHLC; stop-first if stop and target touch in same bar; adverse gap-through stop fill; target fills at target. Only trades with a complete 8h horizon inside their evaluation period are eligible. One ambiguous stop/target exit occurred in initial training; none in final holdout.
- Evaluation: initial 6-calendar-month train; six full expanding-train 2-calendar-month validation folds; final 6-calendar-month holdout (2026-05-14 19:00 to 2026-11-14 19:00 UTC). A 10-hour residual validation stub after fold 6 had zero trades and is not counted as a full validation fold. One frozen rule; no holdout tuning.
- Events/trades: 498 raw events, 93 (18.7%) had >20% hourly OI changes; 394 executed trades across train, six full validation folds and holdout.
- Initial train at 12bp assumed round-trip costs: 88 trades, 28.41% win, PF 0.577, -29.268R net, -33.140R max drawdown, 10-loss streak, 2/7 positive months; moving-block bootstrap mean-R 95% CI [-0.5639, -0.0845].
- Combined six full validation folds at 12bp: 196 trades, 33.16% win, PF 0.636, -56.831R net, -59.490R max drawdown, 9-loss streak. Validation cost sensitivity: 6bp PF 0.765 / -33.006R; 20bp PF 0.498 / -88.597R. Only validation fold 3 was positive (+5.642R, PF 1.278, 32 trades); its block-bootstrap 95% mean-R interval [-0.2454, 0.6431] spans zero. The other five full validation folds were negative.
- Final holdout at 12bp: 110 trades, 35.45% win, PF 0.695, -26.292R net, -0.239R mean, -33.869R max drawdown, 14-loss streak, 1/7 positive months, 6.614% time-in-market, 33 TP / 65 SL / 12 TIME, zero ambiguous exit bars. Moving-block bootstrap mean-R 95% CI [-0.4767, 0.0106]. Cost sensitivity: 6bp PF 0.846 / -11.765R; 20bp PF 0.539 / -45.660R.
- Final holdout monthly 12bp net R: May -1.648, June -0.655, July -8.993, August -4.005, September -13.598, October -0.343, November +2.950 (only 6 November trades). Regime/side: uptrend longs 45 trades / -21.998R; downtrend shorts 36 / -0.897R; range longs 7 / +2.627R; all other side/regime groups were negative. No stable profitable regime was established.
- Decision: reject this frozen OI-contraction/price-impulse continuation rule. It loses in initial training, the aggregate validation folds and final holdout, even at 6bp assumed costs. The single positive validation fold is statistically inconclusive and not enough to override the negative full-sample evidence.
- Important data limitation: Delta's historical OI candle economic unit/sampling convention is not fully documented; a one-time ticker comparison establishes scale compatibility only, not historical sampling semantics. The signal uses relative percentage changes, but this does not remove sampling uncertainty. Funding, spread, market impact and verified fee tier remain excluded. This is a research rejection, not proof that all OI-based strategies are unprofitable.
- Research only; no production logic, main branch, live orders or execution alerts changed.


## Next preregistered BTC experiment — 1h Donchian breakout with completed 4h trend bias (not yet run)

- Distinct from prior 15m Donchian fixed-target and ATR-chandelier matrices: test signal information first on a 1h close breakout, not an exit grid.
- Signal: completed 1h close above the prior 20 completed 1h highs permits a long only when the latest completed 4h close > EMA50 > EMA200. Mirror for shorts below the prior 20 completed 1h lows with 4h close < EMA50 < EMA200. Both channel and 4h regime are strictly prior/completed at the signal timestamp.
- Entry: next 1h open. One event per 8h global cooldown. Forward horizons 1h/4h/8h; no stop/target optimization in this phase.
- Controls: one non-breakout bar per event matched by UTC day, side, completed 4h regime and 3h UTC time bucket, outside +/-1h, no replacement. Report match rate and paired block-bootstrap intervals.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD traded 5m OHLCV, aggregate only complete contiguous 1h/4h bars; preserve gaps and hashes. Calendar windows: initial 6m train, expanding 2m validation folds, final 6m holdout; exclude outcomes crossing period boundaries.
- Costs: 6/12/20bp round-trip assumptions. This is a forward-return signal study, not executable PnL; funding/spread/impact remain excluded and will need explicit treatment before any long-horizon execution test.
- No parameter search; if the breakout fails to beat matched controls in absolute net terms across folds and holdout, reject rather than tune the inspected holdout.
- Status: preregistered only; no code or backtest result yet.


## 2026-11-15 — BTC 1h Donchian breakout with completed 4h trend bias — preregistered

- Distinct hypothesis from prior 15m Donchian fixed-target/ATR-trailing studies: test whether a 1h close breakout has directional information when aligned with a completed 4h EMA trend, before optimizing any exit.
- Signal: 1h close above the prior 20 completed 1h highs permits long only when latest completed 4h close > EMA50 > EMA200; 1h close below prior 20 completed 1h lows permits short only when completed 4h close < EMA50 < EMA200. The channel uses shift(1) and rolling 20 bars, reset at every data gap. The 4h regime is right-labelled and aligned only at/after its close.
- Entry: next 1h open. Global 8-hour cooldown; one event at most per cooldown. Forward-return horizons 1h, 4h, 8h. No stop/target grid in this phase.
- Controls: one non-breakout bar per event, matched on UTC day, side, completed 4h regime and 3-hour UTC time bucket; exclude +/-1h around signal; no replacement. Controls are descriptive observational benchmarks, not causal counterfactuals.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD traded-price 15m OHLCV, two years; aggregate only complete contiguous 1h/4h bars. Preserve gaps and CSV SHA-256. Evaluation windows frozen: development 2024-11-14 to 2025-11-14; validation 2025-11-14 to 2026-05-14 in consecutive 2-month blocks; final holdout 2026-05-14 to 2026-11-14. Outcomes crossing each period end are excluded.
- Costs: 6/12/20 bps round-trip assumptions. Funding, actual fee tier, spread and market impact are not verified. This is an event study, not executable PnL; no trade R, stop/target PF or portfolio drawdown is claimed.
- Diagnostics: event/control counts and match rate; mean/median net forward return, win fraction, PF and event-sequence DD as descriptive return diagnostics; paired mean lift with moving-block bootstrap CI where n>=10; monthly and side/regime breakdown. No tuning against final holdout.
- Implementation: `research/btc_1h_donchian_trend_event_study.py`, workflow `.github/workflows/btc-1h-donchian-trend.yml`; deterministic tests cover prior-only channel, reset at gaps, completed 4h alignment, complete-bar aggregation and next-open/period-boundary outcome indexing.
- Status at preregistration: implementation committed on `ai-quant-rebuild`; dedicated GitHub Actions run pending. No market result is claimed until the workflow and artifact are audited.
- Research only: no production changes, main-branch writes, orders or execution alerts.

## 2026-11-15 — Delta BTC/ETH funding settlement semantics audit (preregistered; pending)

- Motivation: funding was excluded from prior futures backtests because its historical cashflow mechanics and rate units were unresolved. Official Delta documentation now provides a dated mechanism change that must be represented in any funding-aware simulation.
- Current API docs expose historical funding through `FUNDING:<symbol>` candles and the public `funding_rate` WebSocket fields `fi` (interval seconds), `fr` (rate), and `nfr` (next funding realization). Current BTCUSD product metadata reports `rate_exchange_interval=28800`, `funding_clamp_value=0.05`, `contract_value=0.001` BTC, and USD settlement. References: [Delta API docs](https://docs.delta.exchange/), [BTCUSD product API](https://api.india.delta.exchange/v2/products/BTCUSD).
- Delta India guide states that the current 8-hour funding exchange occurs at 05:30, 13:30 and 21:30 IST (00:00, 08:00 and 16:00 UTC), snapshots open positions at settlement, and uses current position value × funding rate. Positive funding means longs pay shorts; negative funding means shorts pay longs. The guide identifies the change as effective 2025-09-08 17:30 IST (12:00 UTC). Reference: [Perpetual Contracts Guide](https://guides.delta.exchange/delta-exchange-india-user-guide/derivatives-guide/docs).
- Delta's 2024 change notice says the funding rate was computed once every 8 hours but cashflow was exchanged every minute under the previous mechanism. Reference: [Summary of changes to funding mechanism](https://global.delta.exchange/support/solutions/articles/80001014551-summary-of-changes-made-to-funding-mechanism-for-perpetual-contracts).
- Therefore, do not apply the current 8-hour position-snapshot model to the pre-transition portion of the two-year archive. The old per-minute cashflow normalization is not yet independently established. Also do not assume the historical candle stamped at a settlement boundary is necessarily the rate charged at that exact boundary until rate-to-settlement alignment is reconciled.
- New audit code: `research/funding_settlement_semantics_audit.py`; workflow: `.github/workflows/funding-semantics-audit.yml`. It downloads BTC/ETH 1h funding history, checks OHLC equality, 8h block flatness, rate-change timestamps, off-grid anomalies, coverage and current product metadata. It does not calculate funding PnL.
- Status at preregistration: code/workflow committed on `ai-quant-rebuild`; deterministic tests and historical audit pending. Funding remains excluded from strategy PnL until the report and settlement mapping are verified.


### Funding archive exploratory inspection — verified against existing archive artifact (2026-11-15)

- Source artifact: [derivatives archive run 43570739333](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43570739333), artifact [14104591074](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43570739333/artifacts/14104591074). Inspected the raw `BTC_funding_1h.csv` and `ETH_funding_1h.csv` files directly; this is an independent analysis of the existing verified archive, not the pending new audit workflow.
- Both series contain 17,495 hourly rows from 2024-11-14 00:00 UTC through 2026-11-14 06:00 UTC, with eight 5-hour gaps each. Every row has `open=high=low=close`, indicating the hourly OHLC columns are repeated rate observations rather than a within-hour range.
- BTC: 892/892 complete pre-transition 8h blocks are flat; 1,289/1,289 complete post-transition blocks are flat. Before the transition, rate changes occurred only at 00:00, 08:00 and 16:00 UTC (483 observed changes); no off-grid changes. After the transition, changes occurred at those same times plus the one expected transition update at 2025-09-08 12:00 UTC.
- ETH: 891/892 complete pre-transition 8h blocks are flat; 1,289/1,289 complete post-transition blocks are flat. ## 2026-11-16 — BTC prior-UTC-day range break/retest — completed, rejected

- Frozen specification: \`research/BTC_PREV_DAY_RANGE_BREAK_RETEST_PREREGISTRATION_2026-11-16.md\`; implementation: \`research/btc_prev_day_range_break_retest_backtest.py\`; workflow: \`.github/workflows/btc-prev-day-range-break-retest.yml\`; successful workflow [43857702736](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43857702736), artifact [14221154137](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43857702736/artifacts/14221154137). Python compilation, deterministic synthetic tests, historical download, backtest, summary and artifact upload all completed successfully.
- Rules: completed 15m close at least 0.10 prior 15m ATR14 beyond the previous completed UTC-day high/low; prior day accepted only if exactly 288 contiguous 5m bars. Within the next 12 5m bars, retest within 0.15 prior 5m ATR14, close back on breakout side and in directional half; next 5m open entry. Cancel on close/entry back inside range or retest penetration beyond tolerance. Stop beyond retest extreme by 0.10 prior 5m ATR; skip risk >2.5 ATR. Fixed 1.5R/2R/3R targets; max 4h; one position per configuration; at most one candidate per UTC day/direction.
- Data: Delta BTCUSD perpetual traded-price 5m OHLCV, 210,240 source / 210,239 valid closed bars; 2024-11-16 11:30 UTC to 2026-11-16 11:20 UTC; 0 duplicates, malformed OHLC, missing volume or 5m gaps; 334 zero-volume bars. 70,079 complete 15m and 4,379 complete 4h bars; 729 complete prior UTC days. Normalized SHA-256 \`bfcaa7a0a081246a9e8ec0dcaca0d01c25e793b250e8399699bddf2c7f0756e3\`. Chronological 70/15/15 split; final 15% untouched.
- Execution: same-bar SL first; adverse stop-gap fill at worse open; target gap at target; data gap before first exit marks path unresolved and reserves the full 4h window. 295 candidate entries; each target had 207 development and 43 validation trades; 0 unresolved paths. Funding excluded because settlement cashflow mapping remains unverified; 6/12/20bp round-trip costs are assumptions; no separate spread, slippage or market-impact model.
- At 12bp, 1.5R: development 207 trades, 33.33% win, PF 0.229, -187.29R, max DD -188.21R, max loss streak 10, positive months 0/18; validation 43 trades, 34.88% win, PF 0.222, -37.30R, DD -38.38R, streak 7, positive months 0/4.
- At 12bp, 2R: development 207 trades, 27.05% win, PF 0.273, -193.33R, DD -194.75R, streak 13, positive months 0/18; validation 43 trades, 34.88% win, PF 0.337, -33.30R, DD -35.88R, streak 7, positive months 0/4.
- At 12bp, 3R: development 207 trades, 23.19% win, PF 0.358, -183.39R, DD -185.80R, streak 21, positive months 1/18; validation 43 trades, 37.21% win, PF 0.559, -23.30R, DD -28.81R, streak 5, positive months 1/4.
- Net R at 6/12/20bp:
  - 1.5R development -94.65/-187.29/-310.82; validation -15.15/-37.30/-66.84.
  - 2R development -100.69/-193.33/-316.86; validation -11.15/-33.30/-62.84.
  - 3R development -90.74/-183.39/-306.92; validation -1.15/-23.30/-52.84.

The 3R development variant was +1.91R gross but -183.39R net at 12bp. Even 6bp left all three development configurations negative; the 3R validation result was still -1.15R.

## 2026-11-16 — BTC/ETH funding-sentiment event study and public-fee recalculation

### Decision
- **Reject negative-funding contrarian-long as tested.** BTC had zero negative-funding events in validation; ETH had 17 and negative net forward returns at the 10bp public-base taker/taker assumption. Development absolute means were also negative at 10bp. All primary matched-control day-cluster confidence intervals crossed zero.
- **Reject the secondary below-neutral 0.01% diagnostic as tested.** Absolute returns were negative in development and validation at 10bp; event frequency changed sharply by asset/sample and validation matching was weak.
- No OOS returns were computed. No stop/target grid was run. No production strategy was promoted.

### Code and report
- Event study: \`research/btc_eth_funding_sentiment_event_study.py\`
- PDRT public-fee recalculation: \`research/recalculate_prev_day_range_break_retest_public_fees.py\`
- Full report: \`research/BTC_ETH_FUNDING_SENTIMENT_AND_PUBLIC_FEE_AUDIT_2026-11-16.md\`
- All files committed on \`ai-quant-rebuild\`; no main/live changes.

### Data
- BTC and ETH Delta traded-price 15m: 70,127 rows each, 2024-11-15 07:30 UTC to 2026-11-15 19:00 UTC, zero gaps. BTC SHA-256 \`d8f210d91553ea43d1ccb0b27bb4d750013e9485c4b007f80647982d1978849a\`; ETH \`f58ce4233c5d7242974e460d811198c03614e113c6097805884ac580ebd8b95a\`.
- BTC and ETH raw funding 1h: 17,531 rows each, 2024-11-14 16:00 UTC to 2026-11-15 02:00 UTC, zero gaps. BTC SHA-256 \`853950ee9193d6e6c0b9ab242fe47c74313d41cbcd9a38cb6255c3d10ed58296\`; ETH \`51eb3ca015985dbfc68891c732f45abee6fa54d32b4085aebf915ec2580aeec8\`.
- Only post-transition blocks were used, beginning 2025-09-09 00:00 UTC; 1,297 common 8h blocks. Chronological 70/15/15 split; development end 2026-07-08 08:00 UTC; validation end 2026-09-11 08:00 UTC. OOS untouched.

### Frozen event rules
- At each 00:00/08:00/16:00 UTC block, use the raw funding 1h candle only after one full hour has elapsed; entry at next 15m open at boundary +1h.
- Primary: raw funding rate <0 -> contrarian long; controls rate >=0.
- Secondary diagnostic: rate <0.01 -> long; controls rate >=0.01. Delta's current guide documents 0.01% per 8h as the interest-rate baseline; diagnostic only.
- 1h and 4h forward returns; 4h exit is boundary +5h, before next 8h settlement at boundary +8h. No funding cashflow applied.
- Latest completed 4h EMA50/EMA200 regime used only for matching. Match same asset, weekday, settlement slot and regime; nearest non-event within 35 days, without replacement; exclude controls within 8h of events.
- Costs: 4/7/10/20bp round trip. Primary 10bp taker/taker; 4bp maker/maker optimistic; 7bp mixed; 20bp stress.

### Results at 10bp
- Negative funding BTC development: 65 events, 47 matched; 1h win 30.77%, PF 0.687, mean -5.44bp, event-sequence DD -704.28bp, max loss streak 10, positive months 1/5; 4h win 40.00%, PF 0.766, mean -6.33bp, DD -694.04bp, streak 7, positive months 1/5. BTC validation: zero events.
- Negative funding ETH development: 94 events, 82 matched; 1h win 38.30%, PF 0.906, mean -2.31bp; 4h win 42.55%, PF 0.962, mean -1.47bp. Validation: 17 events/16 matched; 1h mean -11.00bp, PF 0.552; 4h mean -8.05bp, PF 0.678. All evaluated negative-funding matched-lift CIs crossed zero.
- Below-neutral diagnostic BTC: development 320 events, 1h/4h mean -9.86/-6.59bp; validation 13 events, -15.46/-26.68bp.
- Below-neutral diagnostic ETH: development 270 events, -5.08/-6.29bp; validation 176 events, -9.24/-4.25bp. ETH validation had only 19 matched pairs and too few distinct event dates for a day-cluster CI.
- These are forward-return event statistics, not stop-defined portfolio PF or account drawdown. R is not applicable.

### Public fee anchor and PDRT recalculation
- Current public BTC product metadata: maker 0.02% and taker 0.05% per side. Delta ETH contract page lists the same headline rates. Delta says actual fees can vary by product and user volume/tier. Sources: [BTC product API](https://api.india.delta.exchange/v2/products/BTCUSD), [ETH contract fees](https://www.delta.exchange/futures-guide-ethereum), [fee calculation/tier caveat](https://www.delta.exchange/support/solutions?articleId=80001177869).
- PDRT trade ledgers were recalculated with fee on entry notional plus exit notional, preserving original entries/stops/targets/outcomes.
- 3R development: 207 trades; maker/maker 4bp -59.85R PF 0.695; maker/taker 7bp -106.17R PF 0.537; taker/taker 10bp -152.50R PF 0.418.
- 3R validation: 43 trades; maker/maker 4bp +6.23R PF 1.175; maker/taker 7bp -4.84R PF 0.885; taker/taker 10bp -15.92R PF 0.672. All 1.5R/2R/3R development variants were negative even at 4bp. This confirms PDRT rejection under the public base taker fee.
- Actual user fee tier, spread, market impact and fill quality remain unknown.

### Reproducibility and limitations
- Funding raw archive: [workflow 43671994620 / artifact 14145011170](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43671994620).
- BTC/ETH 15m archive: [workflow 43752078487 / artifact 14176194941](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43752078487).
- PDRT trade ledger: [workflow 43857702736 / artifact 14221154137](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/43857702736).
- Funding rate is used only as a one-hour-lagged sentiment feature, not cashflow; exact historical rate-to-settlement candle mapping remains unresolved. The strategy exits before the next settlement. Flat costs omit spread/impact. Controls are observational. OOS not evaluated.

### Next research step
Do not tune funding thresholds or inspect OOS. Preregister a lower-turnover, wider-structural-risk BTC hypothesis: previous-week range sweep/reclaim on 1h, 15m confirmation, and an 8h maximum hold. This is a new candidate only; it has not been tested.

Funding cashflows excluded; positions capped strictly before next settlement. Spread/slippage/market impact not separately modelled. Missing 15m intervals within planned horizon cause trade exclusion.

- Data: Delta BTCUSD perpetual 5m OHLCV; 210,239 rows, 2024-11-16 11:30 UTC to 2026-11-16 11:20 UTC; zero duplicates, malformed OHLC rows or missing 5m intervals; 334 zero-volume bars retained. Raw CSV SHA-256 `5b612e1c496f16277b459f58ddff33b69d8f7912b70d8f733f0a17be844eb4fb`; canonical DataFrame digest `bfcaa7a0a081246a9e8ec0dcaca0d01c25e793b250e8399699bddf2c7f0756e3`. 620 complete prior UTC profile days in feature prefix.
- Split: development end-exclusive 2026-04-11 12:00 UTC (147,174 rows); validation end-exclusive 2026-07-30 00:00 UTC (178,710 rows loaded). Final 31,529 rows were not loaded; OOS not evaluated.
- Frozen signal: previous complete UTC day's 48-bin candle-volume proxy; short VAH / long VAL rejection with 0.05 prior ATR penetration and close location; next 5m open only if still inside value, POC favourable and reward >=1R. One signal per side/day and one global position. Stop beyond rejection extreme +0.10 prior ATR. Primary target prior-day POC; diagnostics 1.5R/2R/3R. Max 4h; close strictly before next 00/08/16 UTC settlement. Same-bar stop first, adverse stop gap at open, target gap at target, unresolved missing-bar path. Funding cashflows excluded.
- Costs 4/7/10/20bp round trip, 10bp primary; applied to average entry/exit notional. Actual fee tier unverified; separate spread/slippage/impact not modelled.
- 640 candidates; 526 development and 112 validation trades executed per target/cost. At 10bp, POC primary: development 526 trades, 27.76% net win rate, PF 0.372, gross -3.44R, net -408.89R, average -0.777R/trade, max DD -414.56R, losing streak 17, positive months 1/18; validation 112 trades, 20.54% net win rate, PF 0.265, gross -21.63R, net -115.27R, average -1.029R/trade, max DD -115.75R, losing streak 15, positive months 0/4. Exits dev 112 TP/348 SL/66 time; validation 20/84/8.
- POC cost sensitivity 4/7/10/20bp: development -165.62/-287.25/-408.89/-814.33R; validation -59.09/-87.18/-115.27/-208.91R. Gross POC R was already negative in both samples. Median stop risk was 0.156% of entry in development / 0.135% validation; median 10bp cost was 0.64R / 0.74R. Both long and short and all completed-4h regimes were negative.
- Fixed 2R diagnostic at 10bp: development 526 trades, 33.84% win, PF 0.363, net -372.76R, DD -376.10R, streak 14, 1/18 positive months; validation 112 trades, 26.79% win, PF 0.233, net -112.06R, DD -112.06R, streak 13, 0/4 positive months. All fixed targets and all cost sensitivities failed; no post-hoc selection.
- Gate: sample counts passed, but positive net R, PF >=1.05, drawdown and >=50% positive-month gates failed in both development and validation. **Reject; do not inspect OOS, tune validation, or run more candle-volume proxy variants.**
- Next step: pause this proxy family until actual tick/trade-level BTC and ETH data with aggressor side is available for true volume-at-price/delta-at-price research; otherwise move to a genuinely different strategy family. No main, production, approved-strategy, live-alert or exchange-order changes.

## 2026-11-17 — 15m EMA20 pullback/reclaim with completed 4h trend bias — BTC exploratory timing variant + ETH frozen replication; not validated

- Prior-art: the repository already contains a 1h EMA20 pullback event study with completed 4h EMA50/EMA200 regime; that study was underpowered. This run tests a narrower 15m signal-timing variation, not a new independent strategy family. BTC was exploratory; the exact rule was frozen and replicated on ETH without parameter changes.
- Frozen rules: completed 4h close > EMA50 > EMA200 permits longs; completed 4h close < EMA50 < EMA200 permits shorts. On a completed 15m bar, long requires previous close <= previous EMA20, current low within ±0.10 × Wilder ATR(14) of EMA20, current close > EMA20 and > open; short is inverse. Entry is next 15m open (BTC uses first 5m open at that timestamp). One global event per 8h. Fixed 1h/4h forward-return event study; no stop/target fitting.
- Funding handling: evaluation only after 2025-09-08 12:00 UTC transition; exclude 4h outcomes reaching or exceeding next 00:00/08:00/16:00 UTC settlement. Funding cashflows are not applied.
- Controls: one deterministic non-signal control per event, same UTC day, side, completed 4h regime and 3h UTC bucket; without replacement; exclude raw qualifying signals and controls within ±1h of any raw signal. Costs are assumed 4/7/10/20bp round trip; 10bp primary.
- BTC data: Delta BTCUSD 5m traded-price, 210,239 rows, 0 gaps, 334 zero-volume; 2024-11-16 11:30 to 2026-11-16 11:20 UTC; SHA-256 5b612e1c496f16277b459f58ddff33b69d8f7912b70d8f733f0a17be844eb4fb. 23 events, 17 matched controls. Evaluation 2025-09-08 12:00 to 2026-07-16 00:00 UTC; final holdout from 2026-07-16 untouched.
- ETH data: Delta ETHUSD native 15m, 70,127 rows, 0 gaps, 85 zero-volume; 2024-11-15 07:30 to 2026-11-15 19:00 UTC; SHA-256 f58ce4233c5d7242974e460d811198c03614e113c6097805884ac580ebd8b95a. 17 events, 15 matched controls. Evaluation 2025-09-08 12:00 to 2026-07-15 00:00 UTC; final holdout from 2026-07-15 untouched.
- BTC at 10bp: 1h win 35.3%, descriptive fixed-horizon PF 0.753, event mean -2.89bp, control -1.86bp, paired lift -1.03bp (day-cluster 95% CI -20.24 to +19.61); 4h win 41.2%, descriptive PF 2.892, event mean +29.60bp, median -4.09bp, control +0.32bp, lift +29.28bp (CI +4.89 to +55.72; event-mean CI -12.18 to +81.29). Positive months 2/9 at 1h and 5/9 at 4h; positive 2m folds 2/5 and 3/5. Event-sequence DD -103.85/-166.98bp; losing streak 4/5.
- ETH at 10bp: 1h win 40.0%, descriptive PF 0.718, event mean -9.40bp, control -2.17bp, lift -7.23bp (CI -71.23 to +44.69); 4h win 46.7%, descriptive PF 2.283, event mean +35.51bp, median -5.13bp, control -6.49bp, lift +42.00bp (CI -22.31 to +113.22; event-mean CI -21.36 to +104.06). Positive months 3/9 at 1h and 4/9 at 4h; positive folds 2/5 and 3/5. Event-sequence DD -458.62/-328.28bp; losing streak 4/5.
- BTC feed warning: native BTC 15m versus 5m-derived 15m had 19 OHLC mismatches among 70,015 common bars (99.9729% exact); 9 occurred in evaluation. Largest differences: $1,611 open, $517 high, $198 low. BTC used 5m-derived 15m; native 15m retained only for audit. ETH native 15m is not yet reconciled to ETH 5m.
- Interpretation: **not validated**. Both 1h horizons are negative. 4h paired-lift point estimates are positive, but ETH paired-lift CI and both absolute event-mean CIs include zero; samples are only 17/15 matched events, 4h medians are negative and results are concentrated in short/bear events and a heavy right tail. Descriptive PF and event-sequence drawdown are not executable account metrics; R is not applicable. No final holdout/OOS result exists. Do not tune side, stops or targets.
- Deterministic tests passed for complete aggregation, future-perturbation invariance, completed-4h context availability, settlement handling and forward-return direction.
- Full scripts, source CSVs, event/control ledgers, folds, cost sensitivity and mismatch audit are in the run research package. Repository report: research/BTC_ETH_15M_EMA20_4H_PULLBACK_CROSS_ASSET_EVENT_STUDY_2026-11-17.md; ETH preregistration: research/ETH_15M_EMA20_4H_TREND_PULLBACK_REPLICATION_PREREGISTRATION_2026-11-17.md; summary: research/BTC_ETH_15M_EMA20_4H_PULLBACK_CROSS_ASSET_SUMMARY_2026-11-17.json.
- Next step: obtain ETH 5m or trade-level data and reconcile the native 15m series; only then consider a stop-defined execution study. Do not inspect final holdout or tune this sample.
- No production rules, main branch, live alerts or exchange orders changed.

Independent 30,000-resample day-cluster bootstrap: event-mean 95% CI [-21.39,+11.55]bp; paired-lift CI [-37.37,+24.47]bp. No positive 1h effect.

## 2026-11-17/18 — BTC/ETH order-flow zone V2 — completed, all models rejected

- Authoritative workflow [44156642153](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44156642153) completed successfully on code commit `d193af6406ed3ca038a5c6bcafbac8a509a04449`; artifact [14362410468](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44156642153/artifacts/14362410468), SHA-256 `f8011e417ceda144bcde3f5a5c54ff1c6f3df684cd43d35e56c252b0783dda54`. All nine deterministic checks passed, including prior-day-only profiles, profile-volume conservation, Wilder ATR seed/gap reset, funding cutoff, stop-first ambiguity, split-event isolation and no split-boundary truncation. The two-year data download, matrix, summary publication and artifact upload all completed.
- Runtime defect found and fixed before the authoritative run: GitHub Actions installed pandas 3.1.1. `DatetimeIndex.asi8` may use a non-nanosecond resolution, while `Timedelta.value` is nanoseconds; comparing `np.diff(index.asi8)` with `BAR.value` therefore marked every 5m row as a gap and left ATR unavailable. The corrected code compares actual TimedeltaIndex differences directly: `(index[1:] - index[:-1]) != BAR`. True range's first bar in each segment also explicitly falls back to high-low. This is why the earlier V2 runs failed their synthetic ATR test; they produced no market result.
- Data: Binance USD-M perpetual BTCUSDT and ETHUSDT 5m klines, 210,009 valid bars per asset, 2024-11-17 19:15 UTC to 2026-11-16 23:55 UTC; 0 timestamp gaps, 0 duplicates removed, 0 invalid OHLC rows, 5 zero-volume bars per asset; 40 archive files downloaded per asset. Each has 729 complete prior UTC-day profiles, 345 bars without a prior profile. BTC CSV SHA-256 `50cd17b941d786418ccf1bf4512215714ebdc47374b6b0ad2cfadf10b8ae454f`; ETH `2bb2dd3b806d8a33462514e99258a76447863acf30763b2a14cbc2ce380788b6`.
- Chronological partitions by UTC calendar day: development 2024-11-17 to 2026-04-11; validation 2026-04-11 to 2026-07-30; final partition 2026-07-30 to 2026-11-17. **The final partition was already inspected in V1 and is descriptive only, not a fresh untouched holdout.** V2 did not use it for selection.
- Frozen models: VP_ONLY (prior-day 70% value-area edge rejection); TPO_DELTA (5m range-occupancy TPO edge rejection plus opposing bar-level aggressive delta); VP_TPO_DELTA_MSS (VP/TPO boundary confluence, opposing bar-level delta, then a later 5m close through the preceding 3-bar swing). Stops beyond rejection-candle extreme by 0.10 prior ATR; 1.5R/2R/3R targets; 4h maximum hold; one position and at most one trade per side per UTC day/model. Primary 2R / 10bp assumed round-trip cost.
- Primary results at 2R / 10bp, development / validation / historical OOS:
  - BTC VP_ONLY: 579 / 118 / 130 trades; win 29.53% / 20.34% / 29.23%; PF 0.173 / 0.131 / 0.163; net -732.98R / -183.63R / -162.63R; max DD -736.24R / -183.63R / -163.77R; longest loss streak 16 / 13 / 11; positive active months 0% / 0% / 0%.

  - ETH VP_ONLY: 583 / 118 / 122 trades; win 33.45% / 30.51% / 37.70%; PF 0.339 / 0.239 / 0.301; net -417.92R / -110.90R / -109.44R; max DD -419.30R / -111.60R / -113.76R; longest loss streak 13 / 6 / 9; positive active months 0% / 0% / 0%.
  - ETH TPO_DELTA: 233 / 69 / 63 trades; win 32.19% / 18.84% / 22.22%; PF 0.200 / 0.089 / 0.091; net -260.86R / -106.55R / -114.93R; max DD -260.86R / -107.73R / -114.93R; longest loss streak 11 / 12 / 14; positive active months 0% / 0% / 0%.
  - ETH VP_TPO_DELTA_MSS: 35 / 13 / 9 trades; win 40.00% / 46.15% / 0.00%; PF 0.506 / 0.797 / 0.000; net -14.84R / -2.02R / -13.52R; max DD -17.68R / -5.01R / -13.52R; longest loss streak 6 / 3 / 9; positive active months 28.6% / 25% / 0%.
- Cost sensitivity at 2R: every development model/asset remained negative even at 4bp. The only positive validation rows at 4bp/7bp were ETH combined MSS (+3.50R / +0.74R), but each had only 13 trades (below the 20-trade validation minimum) and its matching development was negative (-1.03R at 4bp; -7.94R at 7bp). Its OOS was also negative (-8.58R at 4bp). No model passes development and validation gates at any tested cost (4/7/10/20bp).
- Independent audit: recomputed 2R/10bp trade count, win rate, PF, net R, drawdown and losing streak from `trades_2R_10bps.csv`; these matched the published `metrics.csv` for all 18 asset/model/split groups to rounding. Monthly and regime attribution were inspected; no stable profitable month/regime emerged.
- Decision: **reject VP_ONLY, TPO_DELTA and VP_TPO_DELTA_MSS as tested.** This is a strong rejection of these candle-based proxy definitions, not proof that genuine order flow/footprint methods cannot work. V2 supersedes V1 for the frozen specification; preserve V1 as an audited historical implementation, not primary evidence.
- Important limits: the 48-bin VP allocates 5m candle volume across touched price bins (not trade-by-price); TPO counts 5m candle-range intersections (not standard 30m letter TPO); delta is taker-buy minus taker-sell base volume aggregated per 5m candle (not delta-at-price); absorption is a bar-level proxy. Fees are assumed sensitivities; spread, slippage, market impact and funding cashflows are not modeled. Positions are closed before 8h funding boundaries. Do not describe this as a tick-level footprint backtest.

- Next: close this candle-proxy family and do not retune it. Before another footprint experiment, locate or acquire genuine trade-level price, quantity and aggressor-side data (or an exchange-native volume-at-price feed). If trade-level data is still unavailable, move to a distinct price-only hypothesis rather than label another candle proxy as footprint research.

## BTCUSD 15m RSI(2) extreme-entry mean reversion — completed, rejected (2026-11-18)

- Frozen specification: research/BTC_RSI2_MEAN_REVERSION_PREREGISTRATION_2026-11-18.md. This is a 15m entry-while-extreme variation within the broader RSI mean-reversion family, related to but distinct from the previously rejected 5m RSI crossback system; it is not an independent new strategy family.
- Executed locally against the archived Delta BTCUSD perpetual 5m source retained in the prior Donchian research package. Source SHA-256 5b612e1c496f16277b459f58ddff33b69d8f7912b70d8f733f0a17be844eb4fb; 210,239 source rows, 0 5m gaps, 334 zero-volume bars, 0 invalid OHLC/volume rows; coverage 2024-11-16 11:30 UTC to 2026-11-16 11:20 UTC. Aggregated 70,079 complete UTC-aligned 15m bars (one partial final bucket dropped; 0 15m gaps) and 17,519 complete 1h bars. Derived 15m SHA-256 5cc5a38e08b4299102680089ff90ec8237dcac8d0b96d2dbe4cac61b205f1a09.
- Frozen matrix: Wilder RSI(2) long <=5/10 and short >=95/90; optional latest fully completed 1h close versus 1h EMA200; next 15m open; 1.5x/2.0x Wilder ATR14 stop; 1.5R/2R target; 24h max hold; one position per config, both directions. 16 configurations. Costs 4/7/10/20bp round trip, with net R = gross R - cost_bps / initial stop-risk bps. Same-bar stop first, adverse stop gap fills at open, favourable target gaps capped at target. No extra post-exit cooldown; no overlapping positions.
- Chronological row split: development 49,055 bars, 2024-11-16 11:30 to 2026-04-11 11:15 UTC; validation 10,512 bars, 2026-04-11 11:15 to 2026-07-29 23:15 UTC; final 15% begins 2026-07-29 23:15 UTC and was not simulated. Each dev/validation trade reserved the full 24h horizon inside its own partition.
- Primary 10bp diagnostic (same top net-R row in development and validation): RSI<=5/>=95, completed 1h EMA200 filter, 2 ATR stop, 2R target. Development: 1,001 trades, 34.27% net win, PF 0.752, -192.02R, average -0.192R, median -1.114R, max DD -198.57R, max losing streak 11, positive months 2/18. Validation: 231 trades, 36.80% net win, PF 0.810, -33.52R, average -0.145R, median -1.139R, max DD -39.47R, losing streak 7, positive months 1/4. Both long and short attribution were negative in both partitions.
- Cost sensitivity for that fixed row: development/validation net R at 4bp -78.76/-4.37; 7bp -135.39/-18.94; 10bp -192.02/-33.52; 20bp -380.79/-82.09. Across all 16 configurations, best development net R remained negative at every tested cost (4/7/10/20bp: -62.87/-135.39/-192.02/-380.79R). A validation-only +5.46R row at 4bp had matching development -204.29R, validation PF 1.018 and only 1/4 positive months; it is rejected.
- Gate at 10bp: >=100 development and >=30 validation trades; PF>1.10 and net R>0 in both; >=55% positive active months in both. 0/32 primary rows passed individual gates; 0/16 configs passed both dev and validation; OOS not evaluated.
- Reporting: 128 cost/split/config summary rows; 39,941 trade records at 10bp across 16 independently simulated configs (not unique market signals); monthly and regime outputs retained. Eleven deterministic tests passed. Independent trade-ledger audit recomputed all 128 summary rows with maximum numerical difference 0.0; 32 config/split overlap groups had no overlaps; no OOS trade rows existed. A second clean local replay reproduced summary, monthly and regime outputs exactly.
- Code and full outputs are preserved in the downloadable research package; the local script is research/btc_rsi2_mean_reversion_research.py in that package. GitHub report: quant_discovery/BTC_RSI2_MEAN_REVERSION_RESULTS_2026-11-18.md. Primary summary: quant_discovery/BTC_RSI2_MEAN_REVERSION_PRIMARY_10BPS_2026-11-18.csv.
- Limitations: funding cashflows absent and not modelled despite 24h max hold; 4/7/10/20bp are assumed flat costs, not user-account verified; no separate spread/slippage/market-impact model; R is not account return; source archive ends 2026-11-16 11:20 UTC and was reused rather than refreshed.
- Decision: reject this RSI(2) extreme-entry definition. Do not tune the inspected dev/validation matrix or evaluate the final 15%. Next research should move to an independent source of edge rather than another RSI threshold/entry variation. No production/main/live-alert/order changes.


## 2026-11-18 — BTC 1h EMA crossover continuation — completed, rejected at primary costs

- Prior-art audit: no existing EMA crossover / moving-average cross implementation or ledger entry was found. Existing EMA experiments are pullback/reclaim, EMA51 candle-break or ADX+EMA pullback systems; this tests a distinct cross-entry mechanism.
- Code: \`research/btc_1h_ema_crossover_funding_safe.py\`; run specification: \`research/BTC_1H_EMA_CROSSOVER_FUNDING_SAFE_SPEC_2026-11-18.md\`; report: \`quant_discovery/BTC_1H_EMA_CROSSOVER_FUNDING_SAFE_RESULTS_2026-11-18.md\`; metadata: \`quant_discovery/BTC_1H_EMA_CROSSOVER_FUNDING_SAFE_METADATA_2026-11-18.json\`.
- The executable rules were fixed before the local market simulation; the Markdown specification was archived after the run and is explicitly not represented as a timestamped GitHub preregistration.
- Dataset: archived Delta BTCUSD perpetual 5m OHLCV, 210,239 rows, 2024-11-16 11:30 UTC to 2026-11-16 11:20 UTC; 0 5m gaps, 0 invalid OHLC rows; SHA-256 \`5b612e1c496f16277b459f58ddff33b69d8f7912b70d8f733f0a17be844eb4fb\`. Complete 1h bars: 17,519; complete 4h bars: 4,379.
- Funding source: existing Delta derivatives archive run 43570739333 / artifact 14104591074; BTC funding CSV SHA-256 \`e38d6e4eb3140c81e7a1108b096cd42d5829f50663d64275ac10344767a52dab\`. Evaluated only post-mechanism-transition from 2025-09-08 12:00 UTC. The post-transition hourly funding archive has five 5-hour observation gaps, but all 1,295 expected 00/08/16 UTC settlement observations are present. All positions are exited at a 1h close strictly before the next settlement; no position is open at settlement. The last incomplete funding block is excluded. Funding cashflow is not applied because exact raw rate units/rate-to-settlement mapping remain unresolved; this is a funding-avoidance execution convention, not a full funding PnL model.
- Evaluation span: 10,356 complete 1h bars, 2025-09-08 12:00 UTC to 2026-11-14 00:00 UTC exclusive. Chronological row split: development 7,249 bars (end-exclusive 2026-07-07 13:00 UTC); validation 1,553 bars (end-exclusive 2026-09-10 06:00 UTC); final 1,554 bars reserved and not evaluated.
- Frozen finite matrix: 1h EMA pairs 9/21, 20/50, 50/200; both directions; next-1h-open entry after completed cross; Wilder ATR14 stop at 1.5x/2.5x; fixed 2R/3R target; optional completed 4h close vs 4h EMA200 direction filter; one position/config; max exit at last 1h close strictly before next funding boundary; stop-first ambiguous OHLC; adverse stop gaps at open; favourable target gaps capped; 4/7/10/20bp assumed round-trip costs; 10bp primary. 24 configurations.
- Primary 10bp diagnostic strongest in both splits (EMA20/50, 1.5 ATR, 3R, no 4h filter): development 106 trades, 41.51% net-win rate, PF 0.950, -1.90R, average -0.018R, max DD -11.32R, losing streak 9, positive months 6/11; validation 27 trades, 62.96% net-win rate, PF 2.338, +7.99R, average +0.296R, max DD -2.76R, losing streak 5, positive months 2/3. Exit counts: development 3 TP / 22 SL / 81 TIME; validation 1 TP / 2 SL / 24 TIME. The validation result is small and does not corroborate development.
- Same diagnostic cost sensitivity, development / validation net R: 4bp +5.94 / +10.27; 7bp +2.02 / +9.13; 10bp -1.90 / +7.99; 20bp -14.96 / +4.18. Development break-even cost approximately 8.55bp; 10bp primary result is negative. 9/21 configurations were negative in both splits; 50/200 had only 38/10 trades without the 4h filter and 16/8 with it. The 4h filter did not rescue development.
- Gate at 10bp: >=50 development and >=20 validation trades, PF >1.10 and net R >0 in both, >=50% positive active months in both, max DD >=-20R development and >=-10R validation. **0/24 configurations passed both; OOS not evaluated.**
- Integrity: 10 deterministic test groups passed, including complete-bar aggregation, dropped incomplete bars, 1h/4h future-perturbation invariance, stop-first ambiguity, adverse stop gaps, target-gap caps and settlement-boundary clock. Independent audit recomputed 1,344 metric fields across 192 summary rows with 0 differences. 2,908 trade rows; no overlap, funding-boundary or split-boundary violations. Compact repository runner reproduced all 192 summary rows and every reported metric with max difference 0.0. Local execution only; no GitHub Actions run is claimed.
- Limitations: funding cashflow not applied; 4/7/10/20bp flat cost assumptions; no separate spread/slippage/market impact or user-specific fee tier; 1h OHLC ambiguity resolves stop-first; R is not account return. The final 15% was not inspected.
- Decision: **reject this 1h EMA crossover family under the tested rules**. Do not select or tune the positive validation diagnostic and do not inspect OOS. Next step: pursue genuinely independent trade-level aggressor-side data for footprint/order-flow research; if unavailable at useful historical depth, choose a new price-only hypothesis rather than another EMA parameter variation. No production, main-branch, live-alert or exchange-order changes.


## 2026-11-18 — Binance USD-M BTCUSDT aggTrade historical-data availability probe

- This is a data-source probe, not a strategy backtest. The Binance USD-M BTCUSDT compressed aggTrades API returned 394 records for 2026-11-17 12:00:00.000–12:00:09.999 UTC (IDs 3,529,196,360–3,529,196,753). Observed fields: aggregate ID, price, quantity, first/last underlying trade IDs, millisecond timestamp, buyer-maker flag, and an additional nq field in the connected tool response. Buyer-maker=true indicates the taker was selling; false indicates the taker was buying.
- A 60-second query with limit 1,000 returned 1,000 records but ended at 12:00:25.174 UTC, before the requested interval end. Pagination is required for busy intervals.
- A historical request for 2025-06-17 returned HTTP 400, Binance error -4166: search window restricted to recent two days. The REST endpoint cannot supply the two-year archive.
- Official bulk source: binance/binance-public-data, with daily/monthly USD-M futures aggTrades, python/download-aggTrade.py -t um, and companion SHA-256 CHECKSUM files. References: https://github.com/binance/binance-public-data/blob/master/README.md and https://github.com/binance/binance-public-data/blob/master/python/README.md.
- Data-quality blocker: official issue #525 was still open when inspected on 2026-11-18. It reports missing USD-M aggTrades and derived klines for 2026-10-28 (including BTCUSDT), while raw trades for that day are published; a Nov 2 comment reports the October monthly BTCUSDT aggTrades file also omits that date. Recheck before acquisition. Either reconstruct from raw trades only after validating exact 100ms/same-price/same-taking-side aggregation against adjacent published days, or exclude 2026-10-28 and record the gap. Reference: https://github.com/binance/binance-public-data/issues/525.
- Archive integrity caution: official issue #288 records a disordered 2023-10 BTCUSDT aggTrades file that was regenerated on 2026-10-07. Sort by aggregate-trade ID and verify duplicates/checksums; do not trust archive row order blindly. Reference: https://github.com/binance/binance-public-data/issues/288.
- Audit report: research/BINANCE_AGGTRADE_DATA_AVAILABILITY_AUDIT_2026-11-18.md.
- Status: recent API sample, 1-minute cap and historical API restriction verified. Historical monthly ZIP acquisition/checksum verification and true trade-level backtest are not yet completed. Next step is a one-month 2026-09 Binance Vision USD-M BTCUSDT aggTrades pilot with checksum/schema/order validation; then acquire the two-year archive and patch/exclude known missing dates before constructing true footprint, volume-at-price and delta features. No further candle-volume VP/TPO proxy is to be substituted.
- No production strategy, main-branch, live-alert or exchange-order changes.

### September aggTrades pilot — first run parser correction (2026-11-18)

- First triggered workflow [44292645620](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44292645620) downloaded the official BTCUSDT September aggTrades ZIP (437,374,990 bytes) and its 96-byte CHECKSUM. SHA-256 matched exactly: `252e92894886c2f752744656e4f1d7a278a75e1456aa32442451b07ee9703b80`. Artifact [14421657842](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44292645620/artifacts/14421657842) was downloaded and inspected.
- The first parser stopped before processing data because the official September aggTrades CSV includes a header row: `agg_trade_id,price,quantity,first_trade_id,last_trade_id,transact_time,is_buyer_maker`. The initial parser treated that header as one invalid numeric record. This is an ingestion-format compatibility defect, not evidence of malformed market rows; no trade-level strategy or performance calculation ran.
- Independent ZIP inspection confirmed one CSV member, `BTCUSDT-aggTrades-2026-09.csv`, with 2,394,164,622 uncompressed bytes. The raw archive and checksum were retained in the Actions artifact. The full row scan and 5m reconciliation remain pending.
- Corrected the parser to recognize the exact official header while continuing to support headerless archives; added deterministic tests for both header-present and headerless input. Code commit: `28c4306f19620082c2514fa475db729401b164a5`. Header-aware rerun trigger commit: `5e779821d8055228cfaeb7d309d92c9a7f325042`; run ID/artifact not yet verified at this ledger update.
- No strategy result, archive integrity pass or volume reconciliation pass is claimed until the corrected workflow completes and its metadata/artifact are inspected.

### September aggTrades pilot — full trade-archive scan completed; metadata ordering defect corrected (2026-11-18)

- Workflow run [44294110413](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44294110413), head commit `a271604b504f623e069852039e3230bcfc246bb1`, artifact [14422881922](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44294110413/artifacts/14422881922). The archive download, SHA-256 verification, ZIP CRC check and full chunked aggTrades parser completed. The parser returned without raising any invalid numeric/price/quantity/underlying-ID condition, duplicate or non-increasing aggregate-ID condition, timestamp regression or out-of-month timestamp. The official CSV header was recognized correctly.
- The run then failed before downloading the matching 5m klines because a metadata dictionary referenced `kmeta` before the kline archive had been downloaded/read. This is a reporting/code-order defect after the full aggTrades scan, not a market-data integrity failure. Because the exception occurred before the aggregate metadata update completed, exact row count, ID-gap count and total quantities were not persisted; do not invent them.
- Corrected by moving `kline_zip_validation` metadata assignment until after `read_klines()` returns. Code commit: `caa8cfce8461e6c2e89f494bd27a840df5d59cea`. The new run is pending artifact/report verification. The PR-triggered `deterministic-quant-ci` run [44294424460](https://github.com/undaviyabrijesh-sys/SMC-crypto-alerts/actions/runs/44294424460) has the pilot job skipped because it is a pull_request event; the standalone push-triggered pilot workflow remains the intended execution path.
- No volume reconciliation, full pilot pass, strategy signal or backtest result is claimed yet.
